Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**Admin screens are gated, not just hidden from the menu** - core only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no new permission key, no module change. **THE DEFECT:** `proxy.ts` and `app/cactus-admin/layout.tsx` validate the session and nothing else; the sidebar hid links a role had no key for, and several screens then rendered anyway for anyone signed in who typed the address. Reported live: the Deskwell role **Gazette Author** (holds `gazette.access` + `gazette.author` and nothing else - confirmed against the live database) could open `/hq/config`, and the Roles tab offered nothing to untick because no permission was granting it. It got there legitimately - gazette's `settingsTabs` entry lives on `/config` under `gazette.access` - and then the six core tabs rendered too. **NEW FILE `lib/permissions/section-gate.tsx`**: `denyUnlessAny(keys, what)` - any-of, reads the session itself, returns an alert node or null. **NEW `layout.tsx` in seven admin segments** - `pages/` (any of `pages.read|write|publish|delete`), `media/` (`media.upload|media.delete`), `menus/` (`menus.manage`), `layouts/` (`layouts.manage`), `modules/` (`modules.manage`), `appearance/` (`appearance.manage`), `config/privacy-generator/` (`config.manage`) - a section layout covers its nested routes, so the Puck editors under `pages/[id]` and `layouts/[id]` come with it. **`app/cactus-admin/pages/page.tsx` and `media/page.tsx` were the real leaks**, being server components that queried and rendered page titles, draft statuses, authors and the media library before any API was consulted; `menus`, `layouts` and `modules` are client screens whose APIs already refused, so they leaked structure rather than data. Every `/api/admin/**` route already re-checked - the hole was screens and reads, never write access. **SETTINGS IS THE EXCEPTION AND STAYS ONE.** `/config` is not a `config.manage` screen: a module contributes a tab under its own permission, so `config/page.tsx` gains a door - `canManageConfig || canManageEmailTemplates || canViewMembersGdpr || moduleTabs.length > 0`, else an alert - and passes NEW prop `canManageConfig` to `ConfigPageClient`, which now gates the six core tabs (`visibleCoreTabs`), the tab strip, the page-level Save button, the backup/restore/reset cards, the GDPR consent editor, the Email **Delivery** sub-tab, and the four `config.manage` fetches it would otherwise fire and be refused (`loading` now initialises to `canManageConfig`, so a module-tab holder never sits on "Loading…"). `initialTab` falls back to the first tab the role can actually open rather than General. The members GDPR dashboard moved OUT of the consent-editor block into its own `{tab === 'gdpr' && canViewMembersGdpr}`, and `emailSubTab` defaults to `templates` for a role holding only `emails.templates` - both keys open their own half of a tab and nothing else, which is what those two props were always for. **NEW `lib/permissions/admin-section-gates.test.ts`** - walks every `page.tsx` under `app/cactus-admin` and fails on one that neither checks a permission itself nor sits under a `denyUnlessAny` layout; six deliberate exceptions in `OPEN_PAGES`, each with its reason (dashboard, login, own account, the Inbox host, the Settings door, the module router). It deliberately does NOT count the admin root layout as cover - that layout resolves permissions to build the sidebar, and accepting it passed the whole tree, which is exactly the false pass the first draft of this test gave. Verified in both directions: removing `menus/layout.tsx` fails it. **NAV VISIBILITY IS DISPLAY, NOT AUTHORISATION** - the Navigation editor's `everyone` mode shows a link and grants nothing; wiki corrected accordingly (Deskwell has no visibility overrides saved, so nothing there changes). **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `npm test` **5814 passed / 0 failed** plus 24 new. No SQL, so nothing to run against the live database. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Managing-users.md` (**What a role can reach**, and the corrected `everyone` bullet) and `Architecture-overview.md` (**Admin screen permissions** - the three layers, and why Settings has a door instead of a lock).)

Last updated: 2026-09-07 (**A discussion says who it is from, who it is to, and lands in their post** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION** `modules/unified-inbox/migrations/040_discussion_parties.sql`: `uin_threads.started_by_user_id TEXT` (FK to `User`, `ON DELETE SET NULL`, dropped-then-added rather than guarded with a DO block - a pair of dollar quotes anywhere in a module migration takes the whole module out of the backup round-trip) and `uin_threads.to_user_ids TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT ARRAY[]::TEXT[]`. Both types already have a branch in the core backup serialiser, so `isSupportedUdtName` needs none. Nothing is back-filled: a discussion started before this reads as it did. **THREE DEFECTS, ONE CAUSE** - a discussion recorded WHERE it sat and nothing about WHO it was between. (1) The compose box opened on Subject; (2) the list row read "Unknown sender > <the starter>", because the participant LATERAL join deliberately looks past `direction = 'note'` and every message on a discussion is one, and the right-hand end fell through to the address it sits in, which on the ordinary discussion is the starter's own; (3) it never left the starter's address - whoever it was put to got a bell notice and a tag grant and no sign of it in the post they actually read. **`lib/db.ts`**: `createDiscussionThread` gains required `startedByUserId` and `toUserIds` and writes both (`${...}::text[]` cast on the array - Prisma sends a JS array as `text[]` only when told); NEW `ownInboxIdsForUsers(userIds)` - `kind = 'individual' AND owner_user_id IN (...)`, so only a colleague's OWN address is ever written to and somebody with no address of their own is filed nowhere; NEW `fileThreadInInboxes(threadId, inboxIds)` writes `uin_thread_inboxes` (`unnest(${ids}::text[])`, `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`) and **returns early below two ids** - one address is what every conversation has and rows there would say the same thing at the cost of a join. `threadListQuery` SELECTs the two new columns; `ThreadListRow` gains `startedByUserId: string | null` and `toUserIds: string[]`; `mapThreadListRow` maps them. **`recomputeThreadInboxes` gained a discussion branch and a second guard** - it DELETEs first, so without it merging a discussion and then undoing the merge would quietly file it out of the post of everybody it was put to and nothing on the screen would say so. **A DISCUSSION IS ONE CONVERSATION IN SEVERAL ADDRESSES, NOT A COPY EACH.** `uin_thread_inboxes` is the table a merge writes (031), and every list, tab, unread tally and guest list in the module already reaches a multi-address conversation through it - so nothing had to be taught a second route, and `effectiveInboxIds` reading that list INSTEAD of `inbox_id` is why the starter's own address must be among the ids passed in. **`app/api/discussions/route.ts`**: reads NEW `toUserIds`, drops the caller's own id, resolves their own addresses server-side and files the thread in them - **a request that could name the address would be a request that could file a note in anybody's private post**; everybody on the To line is also notified (union of `toUserIds` and any `mentions`), because being put on a discussion IS being asked about it. **`lib/validation.ts`**: `DiscussionBody.toUserIds` (optional, max 20). **`lib/list.ts`**: NEW pure `discussionFrom(row, staffById)` and `discussionTo(row, staffById)` - null on every other channel, an unresolvable id left out rather than shown, and past two names it is "A, B and 2 others". **`components/admin/inbox/ThreadListView.tsx`**: the row's two ends prefer those, and the avatar asks for the STARTER's picture (`avatarHref('user', ...)`) rather than a person record a discussion has never had. **`components/admin/inbox/DiscussionView.tsx`**: `focusId` is now `uin-discussion-to` when there is anybody to address and Subject only when there is not (a To line that is not drawn is not somewhere focus can go); state renamed `mentions` -> `to` and sent as `toUserIds`; the unsaved-changes guard now counts a picked name. **THE MIGRATION ALSO BACK-FILLS**, which makes it the one statement in this module that EDITS a customer's existing rows: the opening note's author becomes `started_by_user_id` (one `DISTINCT ON (thread_id)` pass, not a correlated subquery per row), the asks raised ON THAT NOTE become `to_user_ids` (somebody tagged in a later reply is deliberately NOT on the To line), and both feed an `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` into `uin_thread_inboxes`. All three guarded (`IS NULL` / `cardinality(...) = 0` / the conflict clause) so picking the file up twice does the work once. It widens nothing - everybody it files to was already granted that one conversation by the ask itself (032) - and without it every discussion started before today would read "Unknown sender" for ever. **A NOTE IS NEVER PUT IN THE IFRAME.** `components/admin/inbox/ThreadPane.tsx` `Message()` gated the frame on `message.hasHtml` alone, so an internal note - whose HTML is `noteHtml(text)`, ie escaped plain text with `<br>` - was rendered in the sandboxed, origin-of-its-own frame built for a STRANGER'S markup (E16). That frame is a document with a white page and a height it has to measure and report back, so a two-line note drew a WHITE BOX on the amber ground with ~400px of nothing under it, waiting on `OPENING_HEIGHT` for a height two lines were never going to fill. Now `kind !== 'note' && message.hasHtml`; a note goes through `MessageText`, ie `.uin-msg-text` (`color: var(--color-text)`, `white-space: pre-wrap`) on `.uin-msg-note`'s `--color-warning-bg` - right in both themes, no CSS change needed. **`MessageText` gained `foldQuoted = true`** and the note branch passes `false`: `splitQuotedText` folding everything after a `>` line is right for mail and would have hidden half of a note behind "Show the earlier messages" on a conversation that has no earlier messages. Header label "Internal note, not sent" -> **"Internal note"**. **THE DISCUSSION BANNER IS GONE.** `components/admin/InboxPanel.tsx` `cannotReplyReason` returned a sentence for `isDiscussion`; that string is the slot a conversation uses to report a PROBLEM (it renders as `.uin-thread-cannot` in ThreadPane and as an `alert alert-info` in Composer), and on a discussion there is no problem to report - the channel is named at the top, every message is marked as a note, and the note line says on its face that nobody outside sees it. Now `null`, which removes it from both places. **THE @ MENU IN THE NOTE LINE IS A DROP-UP.** `.uin-notebar-names` was `flex-wrap` chips wrapping UNDER the box - a shape people read as things already chosen - and the bar is pinned to the bottom of the window, so there is nothing below it to open into. Now `position: absolute; bottom: calc(100% - 1px); left: 1rem`, one name per line, `--shadow-lg` and `--radius-md` like `.uin-suggestions`; `.uin-notebar` is already `position: sticky` so it is the containing block with nothing further said. `.uin-notebar-name` is a full-width borderless row, highlight by `--color-primary-subtle` alone. **A TYPED NAME IS NOW A TAG.** `save()` used `tagged.filter(p => note.includes('@' + p.name))` - picked off the menu ONLY - so "@Emma can you look" typed straight out was silently ignored while the note READ as though Emma had been asked. NEW exported pure `taggedInText(text, staff, picked)` in `NoteBar.tsx`: picked names still in the sentence, plus any `@` at a word start followed by a colleague's whole name, case-insensitive, ending on a non-letter/digit (so "@Samuel" is not Sam), longest match wins ("@Sam Smith" is not Sam AND Sam Smith), and **two colleagues of the same name are skipped** - that is the one thing a spelling cannot settle and the menu can, because a pick carries an id. 11 NEW tests in `components/admin/inbox/notebar-mentions.test.ts`. The full Composer's note mode is untouched: it tags with a chip row, not with @ in the words. **NEW npm script `test:inbox-discussions`** and **NEW `modules/unified-inbox/lib/discussions.live.test.ts`** - 6 tests against a real throwaway Postgres on the OVH VPS, because raw SQL is executed by NO standard gate; the sixth builds a pre-040 discussion, re-applies the migration and asserts the back-fill AND its idempotency. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run` **5850 passed / 0 failed** (10 new in `lib/list.test.ts`, 11 new in `notebar-mentions.test.ts`), `RUN_INBOX_DISCUSSIONS=1` **6 pass**, `npm run test:inbox-mentions` **9 pass**, `npm run test:inbox-merge` **20 pass**, `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` a real PASS (required - a module migration added columns). **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` and the module README rewritten for where a discussion now lands and which field the box opens on.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**A product goes where the cursor is, an enquiry can carry one, and the catalogue stops holding a foot of nothing open** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no new route, no permission change, no core change. **NEW FILE `lib/products/slots.ts`** - the marker a product leaves in the writing: `SLOT_CLASS` (`uin-product-slot`), `refKey`, `refClass`/`parseRefClass`, `priceLine`, `slotHtml`, `slotRefs`, `replaceSlots`, `stripSlots`, `appendSlots`, `flattenWithProducts`. Pure string work, no DOM and no database, so both halves of the wire share it; its own `escapeHtml` rather than core's, because `lib/email/blocks.ts` drags the Puck helpers behind it and this file is built into the browser bundle. **THE REFERENCE TRAVELS IN A CLASS TOKEN** - `uin-ps--<module>--<kind>--<id>` - because `class` is on core's `EMAIL_HTML_ALLOWED_ATTR` and `data-` attributes are not, so a `data-` marker would be silently stripped by `sanitizeEmailHtml` on the way out and every product would stop arriving without a single test going red. `slots.test.ts` asserts a slot is still findable on the far side of the sanitiser - **do not delete that test**. Module names carry no `--` and the kind is one of two words, so an id with hyphens in it (every cuid) reassembles exactly. A slot's inner markup is `img` and `span` only: the send path matches up to the FIRST `</div>`, and a nested block would leave half a preview in the email. **NEW FILE `lib/richtext-blocks.ts`** (`BLOCK_CLASS`, `BLOCK_OFF_CLASS`) so the writing box can remove a block without knowing it is a product and the catalogue can build one without knowing how the box works. **`components/admin/inbox/ProductPreview.tsx` DELETED** - the bordered strip under the box it drew is what this change replaces; `.uin-product-preview*` gone from `styles.tsx`. **`RichText.tsx`**: NEW exported type `RichTextHandle`, NEW optional prop `handleRef` (named for the `react-hooks/immutability` rule, which refuses a prop called `handle` being written to), NEW `insertHtml` on the context and on the handle - focuses, restores the remembered range when it is genuinely inside the box and otherwise collapses to the end, `execCommand('insertHTML', html + '<br />')` so there is somewhere to type after a block. `onBlur` now `rememberSelection()` as well as `emit()`, or a dialog that takes the focus away leaves nowhere to put anything back. NEW `onClick` on the box removes the closest `.uin-richtext-block` when a `.uin-richtext-off` is pressed. **Both composers**: `products` is no longer state - it is `useMemo` over `slotRefs(text)` against a NEW `picked` state, so **the writing decides what is on the message** and a backspaced block is a product taken off, with no second list that could disagree. (It was an effect calling `setProducts`; `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` refuses that.) `onPick` calls `editor.current?.insertHtml(slotHtml(item))`. Initial body runs `appendSlots(body, draftProducts)` when a draft has products but no slots, which migrates every draft written before this. **`lib/compose.ts` `assembleBody`**: `products` changes shape from `{ html, text }` to `{ placed?: Map<refKey, {html,text}>, trailing?: {html,text} | null }`. Slots are swapped **after** `sanitizeEmailHtml`, so what leaves is the table `render.ts` wrote rather than DOMPurify's opinion of it; the text half goes through `flattenWithProducts` so a table is never flattened by `htmlToText`. Anything with no slot still runs on under the writing. `lib/send.ts` splits the resolved products into `placed`/`trailing` on `slotRefs(request.bodyHtml)`. **PROVIDER CHANNELS NOW CARRY PRODUCTS.** `ThreadPane` passed `canAddProducts && !thread.providerModule`, so the one channel where quoting a chair is most obvious - a contact-form enquiry, which is answered BY EMAIL - was the one channel with no button. NEW `productsTravel(channel)` in `ThreadPane.tsx` allows everything except `sms` and `phone`; SMS is excluded because it is billed by the character, not because of the markup. NEW exported `replyWords(bodyHtml, refs)` in `lib/provider-send.ts` resolves fresh and renders each slot with `renderProductText`, trailing products on the end; `app/api/send/route.ts` and `lib/scheduled-send.ts` both call it instead of `htmlToText(body)` (the scheduled path only for `bodyFormat === 'html'`). `sendProviderReply` gains `products` and now `recordLink`s them behind `threadHasLink`, as the email path does - so an enquiry answered with two chairs says so on the context line. Its test file mocks `./db` wholesale, so `recordLink`/`threadHasLink` had to be added to that mock. **`styles.tsx`**: `.uin-modal-card-picker` `height: min(80vh, 44rem)` -> `max-height`, plus `.uin-modal-card-picker .uin-modal-body { overflow: hidden }` and `.uin-product-picker { flex: 0 1 auto; min-height: 0; max-height: none }` so the card is as tall as its results and the list is the only scroller - four results used to sit in the top fifth of a fixed-height card. NEW `.uin-product-slot`, `.uin-ps-thumb`, `.uin-ps-words`, `.uin-ps-opts`, `.uin-ps-price`, `.uin-richtext-off`. The sheet stays `#ffffff` in both themes for the same reason it always did. **A backtick inside a CSS comment in that file is a syntax error** - the whole stylesheet is one template literal. **A BLOCK NEEDS A LINE EITHER SIDE OF IT.** `contenteditable="false"` is what makes a slot one object, and it is also what makes it impossible to write ROUND: a block that starts the box has nothing before it to put a caret in, and two adjacent blocks have nothing between them - clicking above a leading block does nothing in any browser, which is why every serious editor inserts a gap rather than hoping. NEW exported `openUpAround(el)` in `lib/richtext-blocks.ts` puts a `<br />` before any `.uin-richtext-block` that starts its parent or follows another, and after any that ends it; idempotent, because on the second pass the neighbour is the `<br />` the first pass left. Called after every `insertHtml`, after a block is crossed off, and in the effect that writes `value` into the box - which is where a migrated draft arrives with its slots shoulder to shoulder. Nothing is emitted for it: the breaks are somewhere to put a caret, not something anybody wrote. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `npm test` 5797 passed / 0 failed (430 files, 34 skipped) with 17 NEW `lib/products/slots.test.ts` tests, 6 NEW `lib/richtext-blocks.test.ts` (jsdom) and 6 NEW in `provider-send.test.ts`. No new SQL, so nothing to run against the live database. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: **Putting what you sell on a message** rewritten - it goes where your cursor is, and a new paragraph on answering an enquiry, a chat or a WhatsApp message.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Send later stops taking Send & snooze away, and a new conversation keeps the sleep it was promised** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION** `modules/unified-inbox/migrations/039_draft_snooze.sql`: `uin_drafts.snooze_until TIMESTAMP(3)`, `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, no constraint, no index (the queue already has the row in its hand). TIMESTAMP(3) is already in the backup serialiser's set, so `isSupportedUdtName` needs no new branch. Numbered 039 because another session in this shared worktree had taken 038 - check `migrations/` before picking a number. **UI:** `components/admin/inbox/PendingSend.tsx` no longer renders the chase Dropdown - the props `followUp`, `onFollowUp` and `onProblem` are GONE and it is now `{at, onClear, timezone, busy}`; `ComposeView.tsx`'s Send & snooze Dropdown lost its `&& !pendingSendAt` guard and takes `label={pendingSendAt ? 'Send later & snooze' : 'Send & snooze'}` with the matching SnoozePanel `title`, so picking a departure time no longer removes the button. `Composer.tsx` already read that way and is unchanged in that respect. `pendingFollowUp` in BOTH composers is now `const [pendingFollowUp] = useState(...)` - read, never set, so re-saving a draft written when the chase existed keeps the chase it was given. `ScheduleNotice.tsx` gains a required `snoozeUntil: string | null` prop and a sentence for it. **The behaviour change:** a scheduled message answering an existing conversation already survived its send (the queue reads `threadSleep` BEFORE posting and puts it back), but a scheduled message STARTING one had nothing to put to sleep at compose time and the instruction was silently dropped. It now rides on the draft. `Draft.snoozeUntil: Date | null` in `lib/types.ts`; `DraftInput.snoozeUntil` + `mapDraft` + the INSERT column list and BOTH `Prisma.sql` schedule fragments in `saveDraft` (`lib/db.ts`) - it rides with `send_at` exactly as `follow_up_minutes` does, so cancelling the timer clears it; `DraftForComposer.snoozeUntil: string | null` + `forComposer` (`lib/drafts.ts`); `snoozeUntil: z.string().datetime().nullable().optional()` on the draft body (`lib/validation.ts`); parsed, NaN-guarded and echoed back by `app/api/drafts/route.ts`. **`plannedSleepAfterSend` in `lib/follow-up.ts` gains `snoozeUntil?: Date | null`** - the draft's sleep and the sleep the conversation was already having are now folded together (the LATER of the two wins) before the existing chase comparison runs, and each is still ignored if it has run out by the time the message actually leaves. `applyFollowUpAfterSend`'s Pick widens to include `snoozeUntil`, so both live-test callers in `lib/scheduled-send.live.test.ts` were updated. **Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`:** the whole "Chasing it up" section is REMOVED (nothing offers a chase any more), and "Sending it later" and "Send it and put it to bed" say what the two buttons now do. **Gates:** `eslint` clean on every file this change touches; `npm run typecheck` reports ONLY two pre-existing `TS1005` errors at `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/inbox/styles.tsx:3462` from another session's in-flight edit (backticks around `height` inside the CSS template literal) - nothing from this change; `vitest run` on `follow-up.test.ts` + `scheduled.test.ts` **44 pass** (4 new cases); `RUN_INBOX_SCHEDULE_GUARDS=1 vitest run modules/unified-inbox/lib/scheduled-send.live.test.ts` **20 pass** against a real throwaway Postgres, 2 of them new and both executing the changed `saveDraft` SQL and the new column; `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` **4 pass, a real PASS not a skip** (required - a module migration added a column). **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**WhatsApp was being looked for in the wrong country** - `twilio` only, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no new route, no permission change, no core change. **THE DEFECT, in one line:** a WhatsApp sender is registered against the ACCOUNT in whichever Twilio region Meta approved it, and that is NOT moved by the inbound processing region of the identically-numbered phone line - but `TwilioWhatsAppSection.tsx`'s `pick()` copied the chosen site number's region onto `whatsapp_region`, and every read and send then used it. Verified against the live Deskwell account: `tw_settings.whatsapp_region = 'ie1'` (copied from `tw_site_numbers` where `+442081380512` is routed for voice), while both inbound WhatsApp messages to `whatsapp:+442081380512` sit in **us1** - `GET /2010-04-01/Accounts/AC…/Messages.json?To=whatsapp%3A%2B442081380512` returns 2 in us1 and 0 in ie1. Twilio does NOT error on the wrong region; it returns an empty list, so the inbox collected nothing (`uin_threads` had 0 `whatsapp` rows against 108 email / 10 chat / 7 phone) and `windowProblem(null)` refused every reply with "WhatsApp will not carry an ordinary message to somebody who has never written to you". **`lib/whatsapp.ts`**: `WhatsAppMessage` gains **`region: TwilioRegion`** (stamped in `listWhatsAppMessages`, because a message's media can only be fetched from where it was found and a reply can only be posted there); NEW **`listWhatsAppMessagesAcross(sender, regions, limit)`** - `Promise.allSettled` over every region, merge by SID, newest first, capped; one region failing is logged and skipped, ALL of them failing rethrows, because an empty list would be a lie. NEW pure **`regionForParty(messages, party, fallback)`** - the newest message involving that person, else the newest message at all (a brand-new customer still has the sender's own traffic as evidence), else the stored setting. `MAX_MEDIA_LOOKUPS` now applies per region. **`lib/whatsapp-config.ts`**: `getWhatsAppSender()` returns `{ phoneNumber, region, regions }`, `regions` being `getConfiguredRegions()` with the stored one first - the stored `region` is now only a FALLBACK. **`lib/whatsapp-conversation-provider.ts`**: `Grouped` gains `region` (from the newest message in the group), `collect()` sweeps, `send()` posts to `found?.region ?? sender.region`, attachment URLs gain `?region=`. **`app/api/admin/twilio/whatsapp/messages/route.ts`**: GET sweeps; POST now reads ONCE at the top (it was reading twice on the free-text path) and derives `region` from `regionForParty`, used by BOTH the template send and the free-text send. **`app/api/admin/twilio/whatsapp/media/[sid]/[mediaSid]/route.ts`**: takes `?region=` (validated with `isTwilioRegion`), falling back to the stored setting for older links; `_request` is now `request`. **`components/admin/TwilioWhatsAppSection.tsx`**: `pick()` no longer writes the number's region onto the WhatsApp one - that line WAS the bug - `Message` gains `region`, the attachment `href` carries it, and the help text says what the field actually means. **NEW `lib/whatsapp-regions.test.ts`** (4 cases, `vi.mock('./twilio')`): messages found in the region the site did not have written down, both regions merged newest-first, one region failing not hiding the other, and every region failing throwing rather than reporting silence. `lib/whatsapp.test.ts` gains 3 cases for `regionForParty`. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint modules/twilio` clean, the three twilio WhatsApp suites **25 passed**. The diagnosis was made against the LIVE Twilio account and the LIVE Deskwell database, read-only - no writes, no DDL. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The rail stops looking like a grab handle, three of its groups can be put in an order, and a read enquiry stays read** - `unified-inbox` only, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION.** `migrations/037_channel_order.sql`: `uin_settings.channel_order TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::text[]` - the channel keys in the order somebody dragged them into, empty meaning "the order the modules were found in". TEXT[] is already stored by this module, so `lib/backup/serialize.ts` needs no new branch. **NEW ROUTE** `app/api/admin/channels/reorder/route.ts` POST, session + `unifiedinbox.manage`, body `{ keys: string[] }` (`ChannelOrderBody` in `lib/validation.ts`, 1-50 keys, each trimmed 1-100 chars, duplicates refused) - it MERGES rather than replaces, because a channel is governed by the module that owns it and two colleagues can be looking at two different lists. **NEW FILE `lib/provider-read.ts`** `pushProviderRead({ providerModule, externalId })` - calls the owning provider's `markRead` when it has the capability, logs and swallows a refusal. Called from `InboxPanel` (opening a conversation), `app/api/threads/[id]/route.ts` PATCH (only when clearing unread) and `lib/provider-send.ts` (answering one). **`upsertProviderThread`'s unread rule tightened**: was `stored OR EXCLUDED`, now `stored OR (EXCLUDED.unread AND (stored.last_message_at IS NULL OR EXCLUDED.last_message_at > stored.last_message_at))` - the contact form has no read state of its own, so it called every enquiry new for ever and each collection made a read one bold again. **NEW SETTING** `channelOrder: string[]` in `lib/types.ts`, `DEFAULT_SETTINGS`, `getSettings` (`?? []` for a row written before the column) and `updateSettings` (`::text[]`). Not on the settings screen: the rail is where it is said. **NEW PURE HELPERS in `lib/list.ts`**: `sortByChannelOrder(channels, order)` (stable, unnamed channels last) and `mergeOrder(stored, moved)` (moved ids poured back into the slots they already held, unseen ids keep theirs, new ones on the end) - eight unit tests in `lib/list.test.ts`. **`app/api/admin/inboxes/reorder/route.ts` no longer demands the whole list**: it was refusing 409 unless `ids.length === every inbox`, but the rail only ever shows what that person may see and `manage` is deliberately NOT a key to a colleague's personal inbox (`decideInboxAccess`, `lib/access.ts`), so a site with one personal inbox on it could not rearrange its rail at all. It now merges the posted order into `listInboxes()`'s own through `mergeOrder` and 409s only on an id naming no inbox. `reorderInboxes` SQL unchanged. `InboxPanel` sorts the visible, non-hidden channels through `sortByChannelOrder` before the rail sees them. **`NavRail` rebuilt around one `useRailDrag(enabled, move)` hook** - `RailDragState`, `RailDragHandlers`, `rowUnder`, `REORDER_HINT` - with THREE instances (shared addresses, team inboxes, channels), each with its own pair of ids so a row lifted in one list cannot light up a row in another. Team inbox rows and channel rows gained `draggable`/`data-uin-drag`/`data-uin-id`/`data-uin-dragging`/`data-uin-over`; Yours still sits still. The Alt+arrow handler now counts `[data-uin-id]` rows rather than every `a[href]`, which is what makes it correct inside Team inboxes where a colleague's folders hang in the same list. `save`/`move` split into a shared `saveOrder(url, body, undo)` plus `move` (inboxes) and `moveChannel` (channels), each with its own optimistic state and server key. **`.uin-rail-item[data-uin-drag] { cursor: grab }` REMOVED** from `components/admin/inbox/styles.tsx` - the rail is a list of places to go and wears a link's cursor; `:active { cursor: grabbing }` stays, so the hand appears only when a row is actually taken hold of. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `npm test` 428 files / 5766 tests pass, `npm run test:inbox-addressed` **9 pass** (two new: a read enquiry stays read across a re-listing, a genuinely newer message still makes it unread; one new: the channel order survives a settings write), `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` **4 pass, real PASS not a skip**. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: the rail section says which three groups can be dragged and that nothing announces it until you take hold of a row.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**WhatsApp, and a channel stops being the same thing as a module** - `core` + `twilio` + `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. **CORE:** `lib/conversations/types.ts` `ConversationChannel` gains `'whatsapp'` (now `'email' | 'chat' | 'form' | 'phone' | 'sms' | 'whatsapp'`); `components/admin/InboxAllPanel.tsx` `CHANNEL_LABELS.whatsapp = 'WhatsApp'` (the record is keyed on the union, so the label was compulsory rather than optional). No core schema change, no new core route, no permission change. Core 0.5.1539 -> **0.5.1540**, and it ships FIRST - both modules' `requiresCoreVersion` -> `0.5.1540`, because a provider returning `channel: 'whatsapp'` does not typecheck against an older core. **TWILIO 0.1.36 -> 0.1.37.** NEW MODULE MIGRATION `migrations/011_whatsapp.sql`: `tw_settings.whatsapp_enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, `whatsapp_sender TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''` (E.164, NO `whatsapp:` prefix - the prefix is Twilio's addressing and belongs in the code that talks to Twilio), `whatsapp_region TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`, plus NEW TABLE `tw_whatsapp_templates` (`id`, `content_sid` UNIQUE, `label`, `variable_count`, timestamps) added to the manifest `teardown` list. All DDL idempotent. **The sender is a settings row, not a flag on `tw_site_numbers`, and that is load-bearing:** a WhatsApp sender is registered with Meta per number and does not appear in `IncomingPhoneNumbers` capabilities, and Twilio's shared sandbox sender (`+14155238886`, exported as `WHATSAPP_SANDBOX_SENDER`) is Twilio's own number and has no row to flag. `whatsapp_region` is stored rather than derived for the same reason. NEW `lib/whatsapp.ts` - addressing (`toWhatsAppAddress` / `fromWhatsAppAddress` / `isWhatsAppAddress`), the 24-hour window (`WHATSAPP_WINDOW_MS`, `isWindowOpen`, `windowProblem`), sending (`sendWhatsAppText`, `sendWhatsAppTemplate` - variables are passed as an ARRAY and numbered from 1 here, because Twilio's `ContentVariables` is a JSON object keyed `"1"`, `"2"` and a caller counting from zero fills the wrong blanks in a message going to a customer), reading (`listWhatsAppMessages`, `listMessageMedia`, `fetchMessageMedia`) and the SID shape guards (`isContentSid` HX+32, `isMessageSid` `[A-Z]{2}`+32, `isMediaSid` ME+32 - these guard a path built from user input). Media takes ONE FURTHER REQUEST PER MESSAGE (`/Messages/{sid}/Media.json`), so it is capped at `MAX_MEDIA_LOOKUPS = 20` newest-first and a failure costs that message's pictures, never its words. `lib/twilio.ts` now EXPORTS `twilioFetch`, `regionCredentials`, `regionHost` and `authHeader` (module-internal, for `lib/whatsapp.ts` - one REST client, not two). NEW `lib/whatsapp-config.ts` (`getWhatsAppConfig`/`updateWhatsAppConfig`/`configProblem`/`getWhatsAppSender`, templates CRUD, `MAX_TEMPLATES` 100, `MAX_TEMPLATE_VARIABLES` 10) - it writes ONLY the three whatsapp columns and `lib/settings.ts` writes only its own four, because two files full-writing one singleton row is how a save on one tab reverts the other. NEW `lib/whatsapp-conversation-provider.ts` -> `whatsappConversationProvider`, `channel: 'whatsapp'`, `label: 'WhatsApp'`, `capabilities: { reply: true, markRead: false, byIdentity: true, delete: false, block: false }` - delete and block are FALSE on purpose: Twilio's retention owns the messages and inbound WhatsApp never passes through a webhook of ours where anybody could be refused, so claiming either would be a button that cannot do what it says. `send()` checks `windowProblem` BEFORE calling Twilio, because Twilio ACCEPTS an out-of-window free-text message and Meta drops it - the failure mode is "sent" on screen and nothing at the customer's end. NEW ROUTES, all `twilio.manage`: `app/api/admin/twilio/whatsapp/settings` (GET/PUT), `.../templates` (GET/POST/DELETE), `.../messages` (GET log + per-party window state, POST send - free text or a REGISTERED template only, never an arbitrary Content SID), `.../media/[sid]/[mediaSid]` (GET, streams with the region's basic auth, `Cache-Control: private`). NEW `components/admin/TwilioWhatsAppSection.tsx` wired in as a `whatsapp` sub-tab in `TwilioSettingsTab.tsx`'s `SUB_TABS` between Texting and Templates. NEW manifest extension point `core.conversation-provider` id **`twilio-whatsapp`**, `serverOnly: true`, permission `twilio.manage` - the module now publishes TWO providers. NEW `lib/whatsapp.test.ts` (18 cases) and `lib/whatsapp-config.test.ts` (5). **UNIFIED-INBOX 0.1.51 -> 0.1.52. THE STRUCTURAL CHANGE: a channel is a PROVIDER, not a MODULE.** `lib/provider-registry.ts` keyed everything on the module name, so twilio's two providers would have collided - both group by the other party's phone number, so the same external id under one `provider_module` would have had calls and WhatsApp overwriting each other in one row. Identity is now the manifest ENTRY id, which core already requires to be globally unique (`moduleExtensionPointComponents[point]` is keyed on it). `ProviderEntry.id` -> `.key`; `providerForModule` -> **`providerForKey`**; `visibleProviderModules` -> **`visibleChannelKeys`**; `providerPermissionFor(channelKey)`; `ProviderChannel.moduleName` -> **`.key`** (with `moduleName` kept alongside, for saying where a channel came from); `allProviderChannels()` returns `{ key, label }`. `ProviderOutcome` gains `channelKey` and keeps `moduleName`. `lib/provider-sync.ts` writes `providerModule: resolved.id` throughout (`providerThreadState`, `upsertProviderThread`, `insertProviderMessage`, the `woken` event) and reads `watermarks[resolved.id]`; `CHANNELS` gains `'whatsapp'`. Call sites updated: `lib/provider-send.ts`, `lib/access.ts`, `app/api/{drafts,send}/route.ts`, `app/api/messages/[id]/route.ts` and `app/api/threads/[id]/block/route.ts` (both now `providers.find(p => p.id === ...)`), `components/admin/InboxPanel.tsx`, `NavRail.tsx` (`TabChannel.key`), `InboxSearch.tsx`, `settings/types.ts` (`ChannelRow.key`) and `ChannelsPanel.tsx`. **The DATABASE COLUMN stays `uin_threads.provider_module` and every existing row is already correct** - all three shipped providers (`contact-form`, `live-chat`, `twilio`) have an entry id equal to their module name, which is the convention, so nothing re-syncs and no backfill exists. `settings.hidden_channel_modules` likewise holds channel keys and always did. `lib/list.ts` `CHANNEL_LABELS.whatsapp = 'WhatsApp'`. **NEW MODULE MIGRATION `migrations/038_whatsapp_channel.sql`, and it is the one that would have shipped broken:** `uin_threads_channel_check` is a CHECK constraint in the schema, so without widening it to include `'whatsapp'` every WhatsApp conversation would have been REFUSED by Postgres at collection time with `tsc`, `eslint`, `npm test` and the module build gate all green - a CHECK constraint is a string to every one of them. DROP IF EXISTS + ADD (same shape as `030_discussions.sql`, which runs before it and narrows it again on a fresh install); numbered 038 rather than 037 because another session in this shared worktree had already taken 037 for `037_channel_order.sql`; `001_initial.sql` edited in place to match. `uin_messages.channel` has no such constraint and needed nothing. NEW `lib/whatsapp-channel.live.test.ts` - gated on `RUN_INBOX_WHATSAPP=1` + `OVH_*`, provisions a `cactus_rt_uinwa_*` throwaway database, applies core + every unified-inbox migration in order, and proves the four claims that only a real Postgres can answer: `'whatsapp'` is accepted, every older channel still is, an invented channel is REFUSED (so the constraint is genuinely on rather than absent), and the same phone number arriving under `twilio` and under `twilio-whatsapp` is TWO conversations while the same one twice is an update. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `npm test` **5756 passed / 297 skipped, 428 files**, `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` **4 passed** against real throwaway Postgres (required - two module migrations added tables and columns; twilio and unified-inbox both in the built module list), and the new live suite **5 passed**. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Tokens throughout except the product sheet, which is deliberately `#ffffff` with the email's own greys for the same reason the message frame is - it is a picture of what leaves the building. **The reply box's "Throw the draft away" chip is gone; the cross asks instead.** `Composer.tsx`: the `uin-chip` on the action strip, the `asking` state and the whole second `ConfirmDialog` ("Throw this draft away?") are deleted - a destructive button a thumb from Send, asking a question the cross already asks. The existing close dialog gains two conditional strings: with nothing new to save it reads "It is already waiting under Drafts, and here..." with **Leave it as a draft**, and `onConfirm` shuts without a pointless POST. **Throw it away** and **Keep writing** are unchanged, which is the whole point - it is the same three answers the new-message box gives. `ComposeView`'s own chip is untouched; only the reply box was named. **Leaving a half-written reply no longer waits for the save.** `Composer.tsx`: the payload is lifted out of `save()` into NEW `draftPayload(wallClock?, followUp?)` (a `useCallback`, so `save`'s own dep list collapses to `[draftPayload, router]`), and NEW `saveInBackground()` posts it with `keepalive: true`, touching no state and calling no `router.refresh()` - the box it would talk to is off the screen by then, and the refresh would reload wherever somebody has just arrived. The `leavingTo` effect now calls it and `router.push(going)` in the same tick instead of `await save().then(ok => ok && push)`, so a click on a tab is honoured on the spot rather than after a round trip. **One retry, and only on a rejected fetch** - a new draft carries no id, so two requests that both land are two drafts; a dropped line is retried, a refusal is not. Notes are unchanged - they are not drafts, so they still get the question. **Gates:** `eslint modules/unified-inbox` clean, `npx vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 898 passed / 0 failed. `npm run typecheck` was clean at the point this work finished and now reports **two errors that are not this task's** - `modules/twilio/lib/whatsapp.ts(263,46)` and `(276,26)`, `Property 'numMedia' does not exist on type 'WhatsAppMessage'`, from another session's in-flight WhatsApp work in this same shared worktree (`modules/twilio` has ten changed files and an untracked `app/api/admin/twilio/whatsapp/`). Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The picture proxy's own pictures were blocked by the frame's content policy** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - one function signature, one route, one test file. `messageDocumentCsp(nonce)` becomes `messageDocumentCsp(nonce, origins)` and writes `img-src 'self' <origins...> data:`. **Why `'self'` was never going to work:** the message frame is deliberately sandboxed WITHOUT `allow-same-origin` (that is what keeps a stranger's email away from this site's cookies and storage), so its document has an OPAQUE origin - and a CSP `'self'` in a document with no origin of its own matches nothing at all, the proxy's own `/api/m/unified-inbox/messages/<id>/image/<n>` included. Every picture in every message was refused, silently, and came out as a broken image with the failure only visible in the frame's console. `'self'` is kept beside the named origins - it costs nothing and is the right answer if this document is ever served unsandboxed. `app/api/messages/[id]/body/route.ts` passes `[request.nextUrl.origin, originOf(getSiteUrlOrNull())]` - both, because a site reached on more than one name would otherwise work only on whichever one the setting happens to say - through a small local `originOf()` that reduces a configured URL to scheme+host (a CSP source is an origin, and a site URL carrying a path is a source no browser matches) and yields `''` for anything unusable, which the CSP builder filters out. Nothing else in the policy moves: the site is named for images alone, and `default-src 'none'` / `script-src 'nonce-...'` are untouched - there is a test for exactly that. 4 new cases in `message-document.test.ts`, 12 passed. **Found by** the previous entry's change: our own pictures stopped being held back, which is what made a failure that had always been there visible on the messages people look at most. **Gates:** `eslint` clean on the module, `vitest run message-document.test.ts` 12 passed. `npm run typecheck` reports **one error that is not this task's** - `lib/products/render.test.ts:6`, from another session's in-flight product-picker work in this same shared worktree. **NOT verified in a browser** - if a picture is still missing after this, the frame's console says which of the two it is: a CSP refusal names `img-src`, and a proxy refusal is a 502 whose body says why in plain English.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**One phone number can be told to behave exactly like another** - `twilio` only, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION.** `migrations/010_linked_numbers.sql`: `tw_forwarding_rules.follows_phone_sid TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''` plus a partial index on it (`WHERE follows_phone_sid <> ''`), both idempotent; `001_initial.sql` edited in place to match for fresh installs. **THE LINK IS RESOLVED AT CALL TIME, NEVER COPIED.** `lib/forwarding.ts`: `getRuleForNumber` reads the number's own row, and when `follows_phone_sid` is set fetches the leader's row and returns `linkedRule(follower, leader)`. Every runtime consumer already went through `getRuleForNumber` (voice webhook, voicemail webhook, `numbers.ts` `setSiteNumberRegion`), so that one seam gave the whole module the behaviour with no other call site touched. NEW exported pure `linkedRule` - leader's behaviour, **follower's identity** (`id`, `phoneSid`, `phoneNumber`): a missed call on the text line must text back from the text line and name it in the alert email, both of which read `rule.phoneNumber`. It also clears `followsPhoneSid` on the result, so a resolved rule can never be followed a second time. NEW `getRuleBySid` (stored row, link NOT followed) and `getFollowers(leaderSid)`. A leader that has gone from the account leaves the follower on its own stored settings - the follower's values were never overwritten, so that is a real fallback rather than a guess. **ONE HOP ONLY, enforced on save.** `app/api/admin/twilio/forwarding/route.ts` PUT takes `followsPhoneSid` and refuses: itself, a sid with no rule yet, a leader that itself follows something, and any link at all while other numbers follow THIS one. Two consequences that were the actual bugs waiting to happen: a linked number's Twilio voice webhook is set from the **leader's** `enabled || voicemailEnabled`, not its own dormant flags; and a leader's save calls NEW `repointFollowers` to re-point every follower's webhook (per-Region, via `resolveNumberRegion`), because switching the leader's forwarding and voicemail both off otherwise leaves followers answering calls they have no answer for. A follower's webhook failing is logged, not thrown - the number actually being edited saved fine. `forwardTo` validation goes lenient while linked (dormant settings are stored, not deleted, so unlinking gives them back). `app/api/admin/twilio/numbers/route.ts` returns the **stored** `followsPhoneSid`, not the resolved one - that page edits rules. **UI:** `TwilioForwardingSection.tsx` gains a **Call handling** select at the top of each number ("Set up on its own, below" / "Do exactly what X does"); options exclude numbers that themselves follow, the select is disabled while others follow this one (naming them), and when linked the entire Forwarding/Missed calls/Voicemail/Opening hours body is replaced by a note plus an **Edit <leader>** button that just switches the tab. A link pointing at a number no longer on the account renders as unlinked with a note, and saving writes `''` to tidy it. NEW `lib/linked-numbers.test.ts`, 4 tests on `linkedRule` (mocks `@/lib/db/prisma`, which forwarding.ts imports at module load). **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `npx eslint modules/twilio` clean, `npx vitest run modules/twilio` 121 passed, `lib/backup/schema-coverage.test.ts` passed, and `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` **4 passed** against real throwaway Postgres - required, a module migration added a column.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**"Attachments" that were records became "context", and the header line became the links themselves** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no new route, no permission change, no API contract change - two response messages reworded. The module had one word for two things: a FILE on a message, and a RECORD a conversation is about. The second is now called context everywhere it is said out loud; file attachments keep the word, untouched (`AttachmentPicker`, `useAttachmentDrop`, `AttachmentDropChrome`, `uin_attachments`, the **Has something attached** search box, the **Fetch everything as it arrives** setting). **RENAMED** `components/admin/inbox/AttachedRecords.tsx` -> `ContextRecords.tsx` (`git mv`), export `AttachedRecords` -> `ContextRecords`; `ThreadContext.tsx` import updated. **CSS classes renamed** in `components/admin/inbox/styles.tsx`: `.uin-attached` -> `.uin-ctxbar`, `.uin-attached-line` -> `.uin-ctxbar-line`, `.uin-attached-more` -> `.uin-ctxbar-more`, `.uin-attached-menu` -> `.uin-ctxbar-menu` (its 300px width is still repeated as `MENU_WIDTH` in the component - keep them in step); grepped clean of `uin-attached` across the tree. **The header line is now the links.** It was a comma-joined `title`-bearing string of labels with the only clickable copies hidden behind the chevron - two steps to press the thing already on the screen. Each label is now a `next/link` in a `Fragment` with `', '` between, still inside one `.uin-ctxbar-line` with `white-space:nowrap; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis`, so the clipping behaviour is unchanged. **Every context link opens in a new tab** (`target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"`): the header line, the rows in the chevron menu, and `LinkedRecord` in `ContextRail.tsx` on a person's page - following an order out of a conversation is reading it WHILE answering, and a same-tab navigation loses the half-written reply. Adapter section links in `ContextRail` are unchanged. New styles `.uin-ctxbar-line a` (colour inherited, underline at `text-underline-offset: 2px` with `text-decoration-color: var(--color-border-strong)`; hover/focus-visible go to `--color-text` + `currentColor`) and `.uin-ctxbar-source` (`text-decoration: none` - where a conversation came from is not a link). Tokens only, no hex. **Copy:** header empty state "Nothing attached" -> "No context yet"; menu empty "Nothing attached yet." -> "No context on this yet."; chevron `aria-label` "What is attached to this conversation..." -> "The context on this conversation..."; menu `aria-label` -> "Context on this conversation"; `ContextRail` heading "Attached to them" -> "Context" and its empty state -> "No context yet."; `LinkActions` "Attach something" -> **Add context**, "Attach <ref>" -> **Add <ref>**, the remove dialog now says "stops being context for this conversation... you can add it again afterwards", the 401/403 message -> "You are not allowed to change the context here.", the fallback failure -> "That could not be added.", and the no-match hint -> "Add it by its number if you know it is right."; `AddLink`'s internal `attach()` renamed `add()`. `app/api/m/unified-inbox/threads/[id]/links` POST messages: "Type the reference you want to attach." -> "...to add." and "That is already attached." -> "That is already there." **Gates:** `eslint .` clean across the whole tree. `npm run typecheck` leaves ONE error, `InboxPanel.tsx(936,11) TS2741: Property 'sourceLabel' is missing... in type 'ThreadContextView'` - **another session's half-wired feature, not this change**: they added a required `sourceLabel` to `ThreadContextView` and `migrations/036_addressed_conversations.sql` while this was in flight and have not yet fed it from `InboxPanel`. `ContextRecords` accepts and renders `sourceLabel` (plain text, first on the line, never a link, plus a "Came from X." line in the menu) so their prop survives the rename. **Another session is writing in this same module tree** - 66 changed files, untracked `migrations/035_draft_products.sql`, `036_addressed_conversations.sql`, `ProductPicker.tsx`, `lib/products/`. Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The call box says who it is ringing, and the address book answers** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no new route, no permission change. NEW `components/admin/inbox/ContactPhoneField.tsx` (client): a combobox for the **Ring** line on the call modal, reusing the existing `.uin-recipient-field` / `.uin-suggestions` chrome rather than adding any. It reads the module's own `GET /api/m/unified-inbox/people?q&perPage&sort` (already `unifiedinbox.view`-gated, already returns each person's first `phone` identity) and offers only the contacts that HAVE a number - a menu whose entry puts nothing in the box has wasted a click. **It asks only when the typed fragment contains a letter** (`/\p{L}/u`): numbers are filed in international form, so a number typed the way people type one would not match a stored one anyway, and without the guard a dialled number costs a request per keystroke for a menu of nobody. Results are held as `{ term, items }` in ONE state so the menu can prove it is answering the question on screen; clearing them in the effect body is refused by `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect`, which is why the stale list is filtered out at render instead of wiped. The input is `type="text"`, not `tel` - a name is typed in here as often as a number, and `tel` hands a phone a keypad with no letters on it. `CallView.tsx`: title **A call** -> **Make a call**; the Ring row loses `--stack` and its "A number without a country code is taken as +44" hint; the **Call me at** hint loses "Filled in from your account; change it here for this call only" (the "put your number on your account" half stays, for whoever has not); a picked number goes through `toE164` the same as a typed one; and the button strip becomes `uin-composer-row--end` with **Cancel** then **Place call** (was **Ring them** on the left), matching `DiscussionView`. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean across the whole tree. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The writing box rebuilt around its bottom strip, and eight smaller repairs** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema change, no new route, no permission change. **`RichText.tsx` is now three exports rather than one**: `RichText` (a provider holding the box's ref, the saved selection and `exec`/`emit`), `RichTextBox` (the contentEditable itself) and `RichTextTools` (the six buttons + the link line). Only functions cross the context - `attach`, `emit`, `exec`, `rememberSelection`, `restoreSelection` - because `react-hooks/immutability` refuses a ref written to outside the component that made it, which is what a naive split produces; the value-sync effect therefore lives in the provider, not in the box. The colour is now a palette button that opens the seven swatches inline and closes them on a pick (`.uin-rt-ink` loses its frame; `PaletteIcon` is a plain `uin-icon-btn`). **Both composers moved the tools onto the actions strip**: `[paperclip][catalogue][B I palette link lists][Send Later][chips] ... gap ... [Throw the draft away][Save it for then][Send & snooze][Send now]`. New `.uin-send-group` wraps the send buttons as a unit (`margin-left:auto`, `flex-wrap: wrap-reverse`) so a narrow column drops them right-aligned and, when only one fits, keeps **Send now** on the upper line. **`Composer.tsx`:** the `uin-composer-modes` chip row is DELETED (with `changeMode` and the `modes` memo) - mode now comes only from the message's own arrow/menu, and the render-phase `requestedAt` adjustment refills the To line on a reply/reply-all switch instead; new `.uin-composer-head` strip carries `MODE_WORDS[mode]` plus the pop-out (moved off the To line, `.uin-field-pop` deleted) and a new close cross; **Save as a draft is gone**, and so is `Send later & snooze`; the alarm-clock Dropdown is now a `btn btn-secondary btn-sm` labelled **Send Later**; the caught in-app link click no longer raises "Leave this reply?" - a new effect saves the draft and then navigates, and only a failed save stops the journey; the close cross raises a three-answer dialog (Save it as a draft / Throw it away / Keep writing). **`ConfirmDialog` gains an optional `other: { label, destructive?, onClick }`** third button for exactly that. **`ComposeView.tsx`:** same strip, same three-answer question on the cross and on Escape, **Cancel link removed**, Save as a draft removed, and `save()` now returns `Promise<boolean>` so the dialog does not close the screen on a save that failed. **`ComposerOpen.tsx` split**: the context moved to new `composer-open.tsx` (adds `close()`, used by the box's own cross) so `Composer` can read it without importing the module that renders it; `ComposerOpen` re-exports the provider and hook. New `pane-scroll.ts` (`scrollParent`, `pinnedHeader`, `alignToTop`) shared by `ScrollToMessage` and the composer slot, which now aligns the box's top under the sticky header instead of `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})` - the gap in the screenshot. **`Avatar.tsx`: the 404 broken-image fix.** The `<img>` was server-rendered, so the browser fetched, 404'd and drew its own broken glyph BEFORE hydration attached `onError` - which then never fired. It now renders only in the browser, via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeToNothing, () => true, () => false)` (an effect + setState is refused by `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect`). **`MergedFrom.tsx` DELETED**, replaced by `Unmerge.tsx` (`UnmergeButton`, `type ThreadMergeView`): the header panel is gone and the way back out is an **Unmerge** button on the `merged` line inside **What has been done to this**, paired by new `undoableFromEvent(event, merges)` matching `event.detail.mergeIds` against the still-undoable list; `InboxPanel` now passes `{ id, subject }` only. **`ThreadPane.tsx`:** subject gains `title` (the whole of it on hover) and `.uin-thread-subject` becomes `flex: 1 1 0` - `auto` was why a long subject pushed the snooze/assign/status/close row onto its own line. **`NoteBar.tsx` rewritten around typing @**: exported pure `mentionQueryAt(text, caret)`, a name menu under the bar with arrow-key/Return/Escape handling, names picked (never scraped) and re-checked against the sentence at save time, and the **@ button and its chip wall are gone** (`.uin-notebar-tag`, `-tag-count`, `-tags`, `-find` styles deleted, `-names`/`-name` added). Relative times in `ThreadListView`, `SentListView`, `DraftListView` and `MentionListView` gained `title={formatFull(...)}` to match the conversation's own. **A scheduled send no longer wakes a conversation somebody put to sleep**: new `threadSleep(threadId)` in `lib/db.ts` (raw SELECT of status + snooze_until), read in `scheduled-send.ts` BEFORE the send and passed to `applyFollowUpAfterSend(draft, threadId, sentAt, was)`; `follow-up.ts` gains pure `plannedSleepAfterSend({ followUpMinutes, sentAt, was })` returning `{ until, chase }` - the later of a chase and an unexpired snooze wins, and only a chase reassigns the conversation or writes the `awaiting` event. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 893 passed, with 8 new `lib/follow-up.test.ts` cases and 8 new `components/admin/inbox/notebar-mentions.test.ts` cases around the exported `mentionQueryAt`. **Live, for real, no skip:** `RUN_INBOX_REOPEN_GUARDS=1` reopen-on-reply suite 9 passed against a real throwaway `cactus_rt_*` database on the OVH VPS, including three new assertions that execute the new `threadSleep` SQL. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. **Another session was writing in this same module tree throughout** (a phone lookup for `CallView`, new untracked `ContactPhoneField.tsx`, plus `NavRail`/`CheckNowButton`/campaign panels); nothing of theirs was touched, and the one lint error left in the tree at the end of this is in their in-progress file, not in any of the files listed here. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: **Making it look like something**, **Drafts**, **Sending it later**, **Who a reply goes to**, **Writing a new one**, **Attachments**, **Asking a colleague to look at something** and **Merging conversations into one** all rewritten for the above.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**A draft is private to whoever wrote it again** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree.
