Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Products on a message: pick one out of the catalogue, print it under the writing, attach it to the conversation** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. **ONE NEW MODULE MIGRATION.** `migrations/035_draft_products.sql`: `uin_drafts.products JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb`, idempotent, references only (`{moduleName, kind, id}`) and never a name or a price. **NEW SEAM `lib/products/`,** written under the context rail's three rules (reads only, raw SQL only, gated before it costs anything): `types.ts` (`ProductRef`, `ProductChoice`, `ProductLink`, `ResolvedProduct`, `ProductSource`, `MAX_PRODUCTS_PER_MESSAGE` 20, `PRODUCT_SEARCH_LIMIT` 12, `VARIATION_LIMIT` 200 - no runtime imports, so a client component may `import type` from it); `price.ts` (pure: `displayIncludesTax`, `displayAmount`, `taxSuffix`, `formatPrice`, `effectivePrice`, `DEFAULT_TAX_LABEL`, `PLAIN_PRICE_DISPLAY` - shop's rules restated rather than imported, 19 tests); `shop.ts` (`shopProducts`, permission `shop.products`, tables `shp_products`/`shp_product_media`/`shp_settings`, with `svr_*` probed at the point of use so shop-variations is optional); `render.ts` (`renderProductTable`, `renderProductText`, `priceLine`, 16 tests); `index.ts` (`PRODUCT_SOURCES`, `usableProductSources`, `canAddProducts`, `searchProducts`, `listVariations`, `resolveProducts`). **THE TAX SUFFIX RULE:** "+ VAT" appears when the storefront quotes NET prices AND the product's tax class carries a rate in the shop's default zone - both halves, or it is a lie about the money. The words come from shop's `invoiceTaxLabel` (`+ ${label}`), deliberately NOT its storefront `priceDisplayTaxSuffix`, which on a net shop reads "ex. VAT": a shelf says what the figure IS, one price quoted to one person says what will happen to it. A listing with variations is quoted from its cheapest enabled variation with "From" only where there is a real spread (half a penny of tolerance); a zero price is quoted as **no price at all** rather than "£0.00". **NEW ROUTE** `app/api/products/route.ts` (GET, `unifiedinbox.reply` + the source's own permission): `?q=` searches/browses, `?of=<id>&module=<name>` lists that listing's variations. **SEND PATH.** `SendBody.products` / `DraftBody.products` (Zod `ProductRefBody`, max 20); `SendRequest.products` -> `resolveProducts` -> `assembleBody({ products })`, which now places them **under the writing and above the signature** and NOT through `sanitizeEmailHtml` (built from our own tables, and email layout is exactly what a stricter pass flattens); every resolved product is then `recordLink`ed to the thread as `recordType` `product`/`variation`, `threadHasLink` first so the same chair twice is one row. `lib/record-links.ts` gains those two types and `m/shop/products/<id>` for both. `scheduled-send.ts` passes `draft.products`, so a message set for Monday quotes Monday's prices. **THE TABLE:** no heading row, no quantity column, name + options + price, picture column only when something has one, rule on the cells not the row (Outlook), everything escaped, plain-text half written separately rather than flattened. **UI.** NEW `components/admin/inbox/ProductPicker.tsx` (`ProductPicker`, `ProductChips`, `productKey`, `priceLabel`): search box, listings with a 40px thumbnail, **Choose one of N** folding the variations in under the listing, and the panel stays open after a pick because quoting three chairs is one errand. NEW `TagIcon`. Both composers gain `canAddProducts` + `draftProducts` props, a tag button beside the paperclip, chips beside the file chips, and `products` in both payloads - `Composer.tsx` strips them from a note, and `ThreadPane` passes `canAddProducts && !thread.providerModule` so a chat or SMS reply never offers a table it cannot carry. `DraftReadOnlyView` names a colleague's products without linking them, as it already does their files. `InboxPanel` resolves a draft's references server-side (one extra call only when a draft has any) and answers `canAddProducts` from the session. New styles `.uin-product-picker`, `.uin-product-row`, `.uin-product-words`, `.uin-product-thumb`, `.uin-product-open`, `.uin-product-variations` - tokens only. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `vitest run` 5716 passed / 0 failed. **Live, for real, no skip:** `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` 4 passed against a real throwaway `cactus_rt_*` database on the OVH VPS with the new column in the schema, and **every raw query in `lib/products/shop.ts` was executed against the live Deskwell database read-only** (`psql`, SELECTs only) - the search both ways, the from-price aggregate, the variations `string_agg` and both resolve queries - because no typecheck, lint or build ever parses SQL. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: NEW **Putting what you sell on a message** section under Attachments, plus a paragraph under **What the conversation is about**.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Our own pictures are no longer held back** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - one new pure function, three edited files. NEW `isOwnSender(message, gate)` in `modules/unified-inbox/lib/people.ts`, beside `shouldBecomePerson` and taking the same `PersonGate`: `direction !== 'in'` is ours by definition (an outbound message and an internal note both), and an inbound one is ours when `identityKey(fromAddress)` is in `ownAddresses` or `staffAddresses`, or its `domainOf` is in `ownDomains`. **From decides it, never Reply-To** - Reply-To is set by whoever sent the message, so trusting it would let anybody claim to be us; From can be forged too and a forged own-domain sender gets its pictures fetched by the site, which is the cost of the setting and small next to what the reader was pressing the button on anyway. 8 new cases in `people.test.ts`. `InboxPanel.tsx` builds the gate inline in the thread branch from what that page already holds - `allInboxes`, `staffRows`, `settings` - so recognising our own post costs **no extra query**; `staffRows`' select gains `email` (read only to match an address, never put on the page). `ThreadMessageView` gains `ownSender: boolean`, set on the map that already attaches the files. `ThreadPane` passes it to `MessageBody`, whose new `ownSender` prop seeds `useState(showImages)` and widens the restore effect to `setShowImages(ownSender || hasShownImages(messageId))` - so the notice, which is already gated on `!showImages`, never appears on our own post and the frame loads with `?images=1` first time. The remembering in `localStorage` is untouched and still covers strangers. **Deliberately NOT enforced in `app/api/messages/[id]/body/route.ts`:** `?images=1` is the reader's own choice and has always been theirs to make from the button, so defaulting it server-side would buy nothing and cost three queries per frame. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `vitest run` on `people.test.ts` 36 passed. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` "Reading email safely" gains the **Your own post is never held back** paragraph.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The status menu now takes only the width of what is in it, and marking a conversation done offers five seconds of undo** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - one new component, three edited components, one stylesheet. `Dropdown.tsx`: `width` is **optional now, with no 240 default** - left off, the panel is a `position: fixed` box with no width set and therefore shrink-to-fit. Placement had to follow: `place()` takes `(height, panelWidth)` instead of reading `width` off the props, because an `align="end"` panel is positioned from its own right-hand edge and a self-sizing panel does not know that edge until it is on the page. The `useLayoutEffect` now passes `panel.current?.offsetWidth`; the click handler guesses with `trigger.current?.offsetWidth` and is corrected on the same frame, before paint. Every other call site still passes an explicit width, so nothing else moves. `ThreadActions.tsx` drops `width={200}` from the status dropdown - two one-word answers in a 200px panel was the complaint. NEW `components/admin/inbox/UndoToast.tsx` ('use client'): `HOLD_MS = 5000` then `FADE_MS = 300` of opacity transition driven by a `data-going` attribute, `role="status" aria-live="polite"`, bottom centre of the WINDOW (`position: fixed`, z-index 10001 - above `.uin-menu`'s 10000) because every pane on this screen scrolls its own contents. `onDone` is held in a ref and **written in its own effect, not during render** - `react-hooks/refs` is a hard error in this repo and caught it. `ThreadActions` gains `undoTo`, which holds the whole PATCH body rather than a status string: undoing a done on something that was SNOOZED has to send `snoozeUntil` back with it, because `app/api/threads/[id]/route.ts` rejects `status: 'snoozed'` with no date (`'Say when it should come back.'`, 400). That is why `ThreadActions` gains a required `snoozeUntil: string | null` prop, passed from `ThreadPane` as `thread.snoozeUntil?.toISOString() ?? null` (ISO string, not a `Date` across the RSC boundary). `patch()` now returns a boolean so the toast only appears once the site has agreed the change happened. Picking **Open** from the menu clears the toast. The bulk **Mark as done** in `ThreadListView` deliberately does NOT get one - it was not asked for and undoing a batch means restoring each row's own previous status. New styles `.uin-toast`, `.uin-toast-text`, `.uin-toast-undo` + a `prefers-reduced-motion` rule that drops the transition only; tokens only, no hex. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean. **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: one new paragraph under "Where it stands is a button".)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Both writing boxes rebuilt: an editable To line, Bcc, a subject you can change, a pop-out, formatting, and the send buttons regrouped** - core + `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. **TWO NEW MODULE MIGRATIONS.** `migrations/033_bcc.sql`: `uin_messages.bcc_addresses` and `uin_drafts.bcc_addresses`, both `TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT ARRAY[]::TEXT[]`. `migrations/034_draft_body_format.sql`: `uin_drafts.body_format TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'text'` + `uin_drafts_body_format_check` (`'text'`/`'html'`). Both idempotent, no dollar quoting. **CORE.** `lib/email/index.ts`: `EmailPayload.bcc?: string[]`, threaded to Brevo (`bcc: [{email}]`) and to nodemailer (`bcc: 'a, b'`) - its own list, never folded into `cc`, which is the whole of what a blind copy is. `lib/sanitize-config.ts`: `b` and `i` added to `EMAIL_HTML_ALLOWED_TAGS` only (NOT to the richtext list) - a contentEditable box's bold is `<b>` in every browser, and stripping it silently unbolds what somebody plainly bolded. 3 new cases in `lib/email/send.test.ts`. **MODULE, BCC END TO END.** `SendBody.bcc` / `DraftBody.bcc` (Zod, max 50); `SendRequest.bcc` -> `prepareSend` (validated with to/cc, and **never derived from the conversation whatever the mode** - nobody was blind-copied by mail we can read) -> `insertOutboundMessage.bccAddresses` (optional, `?? []`) -> `SendableMessage.bcc` -> `deliver` -> core; `RawMessageInput.bcc` so the IMAP Sent copy is honest; `retrySend` reads `message.bccAddresses` back off the row; `scheduled-send.ts` passes `draft.bcc`. `Draft.bcc`, `DraftForComposer.bcc`, `DraftInput.bcc?`, `isWorthSaving` counts one. 4 new cases in `lib/send.test.ts`. **MODULE, RICH TEXT.** NEW `components/admin/inbox/RichText.tsx` - contentEditable + `document.execCommand`, deliberately no new dependency: bold, italic, seven colours, link, bullet list, numbered list, Ctrl/Cmd+B and +I. `styleWithCSS` false for bold/italic (`<b>`/`<i>`, what email is made of) and true for colour (`<span style="color:...">`); buttons `preventDefault` on **mousedown** or the selection they act on is gone before the click; the box is UNCONTROLLED and written into only when `value` differs, or the caret jumps to the front on every keystroke; paste is forced to `text/plain`. `Draft.bodyFormat` (`'text'|'html'`) says which of two things the body column holds - **a column, not a guess at the content**, or "a < b" renders as broken markup and a pasted line of HTML sends as live markup. Old drafts open through `plainTextToHtml` and save back as markup. NEW pure `draftBodyText()` + `htmlHasWriting()` in `lib/drafts.ts` (11 new cases) used by the Drafts list, the read-only view of a colleague's draft, the scheduled sender's provider-reply text and every "is there anything written" test. **The drafts route sanitises on the way IN** (`sanitizeEmailHtml`) - a draft is readable by whoever can read the address and is put back into another person's writing box, so cleaning it on the way out would leave the row a loaded gun for anything that ever read it without remembering to. **MODULE, THE TWO COMPOSERS.** `Composer.tsx`: To is a `RecipientField` seeded from the server's `replyTo`/`replyAllTo` and refilled as the mode chips change **until somebody edits it**; separate `forwardTo` bucket, because a forward must never inherit the customer's address. Cc / Bcc / Subject are links at the end of the To row, each opening its own `uin-field-row`; Subject opens on the server-computed default (NEW `replySubject`/`forwardSubject` props, worked out in `InboxPanel` from `newest?.subject ?? thread.subject` - the same fallback `prepareSend` uses). NEW pop-out: `createPortal` into `document.body` behind `poppedOut` state, so the component and everything typed in it survive the move; body scroll locked, Escape collapses. `ComposeView.tsx` gets the same bottom strip, the same Bcc line and `submit(snoozeUntil?)` which PATCHes the thread the send just created. **Send button strip, both boxes:** paperclip icon + alarm icon on the left, `.uin-composer-gap` (margin auto), then Throw away / Save as a draft / [Send later, Send later & snooze] / Send & snooze / **Send now**. Send & snooze and Send later & snooze open `SnoozePanel` with a `title` prop; there is deliberately NO Send later & snooze on the new-message box - a message that has not gone has not started a conversation, and the chase covers that case. NEW `SendLaterPanel.tsx` (alarm-clock menu, same words and same `snoozeOptions` as the conversation's own clock, plus Cancel the timer), NEW `PendingSend.tsx` (the picked-but-not-committed time + the follow-up chase, shared by both boxes), NEW `Calendar.tsx` (`TimeCalendar` + `landsOn`, lifted out of `SnoozePanel` so two menus share one month), NEW `ScheduleNotice.tsx` (the failed / held / scheduled sentences). **`SendLater.tsx` DELETED** - its picker is the alarm menu, its chase is `PendingSend`, its notices are `ScheduleNotice`; nothing it did was dropped. `minSendAt` removed from `Composer`/`ComposerSlot`/`ThreadPane`/`ComposeView`/`InboxPanel` with the datetime-local box that wanted it. NEW icons: `ExpandIcon`, `CollapseIcon`, `LinkIcon`, `ListIcon`, `NumberedListIcon`, `PaletteIcon`. `styles.tsx`: `.uin-richtext*`, `.uin-rt-*`, `.uin-field-links`, `.uin-field-pop`, `.uin-composer-actions`, `.uin-composer-gap`, `.uin-pending-send`, `.uin-mention-search`, `.uin-composer-aside`, `.uin-composer > .uin-fields`; `.uin-sendlater*` removed. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint .` clean whole repo, `vitest run` 5668 passed / 0 failed. **Live, for real, no skip:** `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` 4 passed against a real throwaway `cactus_rt_*` database on the OVH VPS (run twice - after 033 and again after 034), and `RUN_INBOX_SCHEDULE_GUARDS=1 RUN_INBOX_UPLOAD_GUARDS=1` 24 passed, which is what actually executes the edited `saveDraft` and `insertOutboundMessage` SQL. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: NEW **Who a reply goes to**, **Making it look like something** and **Send it and put it to bed** sections; **Writing a new one**, **Sending it later**, **Chasing it up** and **Attachments** rewritten. **Message header rebuilt as two lines** (`unified-inbox`, same uncommitted tree): `ThreadPane.tsx` `MessageHeader` now draws the sender's name and their address on one line and a NEW `ToLine` (`To: …`) under it, with the picture standing beside both. The inbound and outbound arrows are gone from the header, as are the words "Received from"/"Sent to" - kept for a screen reader as `.sr-only`. NEW `components/admin/inbox/AddressLine.tsx` (client): clips an address with an ellipsis and shows the core `AdminTooltip` with the whole of it ONLY when it is actually clipped, measured with a `ResizeObserver`. `.uin-msg-head` no longer wraps, so the time, the dots and the reply arrow can never drop onto a row of their own again; new `.uin-msg-head-lines`, `.uin-msg-head-line`, `.uin-msg-head-to`, `.uin-msg-dir-label`, `.uin-msg-address`, `.uin-msg-address-text` in `styles.tsx`, head avatar 1.5rem -> 2rem. `ComposerOpen.tsx` `ComposerSlot` scrolls the writing box into view when Reply/Reply all/Forward opens or switches it (not on the way in, where a saved draft opens it and the pane has its own opening position). **NOT verified in a browser** - visual checking is Chris's.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Reading oldest first, a conversation now opens on the message worth reading rather than at the top** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - one new client component, two edited components, one settings hint. NEW `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/inbox/ScrollToMessage.tsx`: a client island that draws nothing and takes `threadId` + `targetId`. It walks OUT from the message for the thing that actually scrolls (first ancestor whose computed `overflow-y` is `auto`/`scroll`; null means the window - the reading pane scrolls its own contents at >=900px and the whole page scrolls below it), asks whether `.uin-thread-head` is `position: sticky` before subtracting its height (pinned only at >=900px, and aligning to a scroller with an opaque band across it hides the message's own header), and then corrects `scrollTop`. **Corrects repeatedly, not once:** an email's HTML lives in a frame that reports its height back and keeps reporting it as pictures arrive (`MessageBody`, `OPENING_HEIGHT = 400`), so a thread of large messages settles a second or two after it is drawn and a single scroll on mount lands nowhere near. A `ResizeObserver` on `.uin-thread-body` re-aligns; `SETTLE_MS = 6000` and the first `wheel`/`touchstart`/`keydown`/`pointerdown` stop it for good. Deliberately NOT listening for `scroll` - that is what the correction itself fires. **Server decides which message**, because it turns on whether the conversation was unread when it was opened and that stops being true one line later: `InboxPanel` captures `wasUnread` BEFORE `setThreadRead(thread.id, false)` and computes `openOnMessage` = null when `settings.newestFirst` (newest is already at the top), else the newest `direction === 'in'` message when it was unread, else the last message of any kind - an internal note included. `ThreadPane` gains the `scrollToMessageId` prop, a `messageDomId()` helper, an `id` on every `<article className="uin-msg ...">`, and renders the island keyed on `thread.id` inside `.uin-thread-body` - suppressed when a draft is opening the writing box, since somebody returning to a half-written reply came for the box. `CollectingPanel` hint for **Show the newest message at the top of a conversation** reworded (its old selling point, not scrolling to find what was last said, is now what the OFF setting does too). Module README "Reading and replying" and core wiki `Unified-Inbox.md` -> "Which way round a conversation reads" both updated. **Gates:** `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint .` clean, whole repo.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**An individual inbox can be shared with named colleagues, and the rail grew a TEAM INBOXES group** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. **NO schema change and no migration** - `uin_inbox_access` already had `(inbox_id, user_id, can_reply)`; it was simply never consulted on an individual inbox. **ACCESS RULE, `lib/access.ts`.** `decideInboxAccess` on `kind = 'individual'` now: no owner -> `manage` fallback (unchanged); owner -> as before; **anybody else -> in only if they hold a row**, with `reply = row.canReply && (canReply || canManage)`. `manage` is still NOT a way past that list, which is the point of the kind. The four call sites that did `inbox.kind === 'individual' ? [] : await listInboxAccess(id)` all now read the rows. `audienceForSave` keeps the owner (forced `canReply: true`, de-duped) and KEEPS the entries the form sent beside them; `defaultUserIds` is still forced to `[owner]` - a guest covering somebody's post is not moved into their inbox. `access.test.ts` rewritten for the new branch, 3 new `audienceForSave` cases. **URL, `lib/list.ts`.** `inbox` param gains three scoped forms - `sent:<inboxId>`, `drafts:<inboxId>`, `mentions:<inboxId>` - parsed by NEW `readFolder()` into NEW `InboxParams.folderInboxId`; `inboxId` explicitly excludes them, or `sent:in1` reads as an address called "sent:in1". `sent:` with nothing after it degrades to the plain folder. `searchableScope` unwraps a scoped folder to its address. `splitInboxes` now takes `meUserId` and returns a THIRD group, `team` - individual inboxes whose `ownerUserId` is not the reader's, orphans included (an address whose owner's account has gone is nobody's, so it is not "Yours"). `default-inbox.test.ts` updated + 2 new cases; `list.test.ts` + 5 new parse cases. **SQL, `lib/db.ts`.** NEW `mentionScope(userId, inboxId)` using the existing `inboxMatch` (so a merged conversation is in both addresses' folders); `listMentions` gains `inboxId?`, `countMentions`/`mentionStatusCounts` gain a third/second arg and now `JOIN uin_threads t`. `draftScope` gains `includeUnfiled` (off for a scoped folder, or the reader's own chat drafts turn up in a colleague's Drafts); `listDrafts`/`countDrafts` thread it through. **UI.** `NavRail.tsx`: `TabInbox` gains `ownerUserId` + `ownerName`; NEW **Team inboxes** group, each row a `.uin-rail-branch` of a `<button class="uin-rail-twist" aria-expanded aria-controls>` beside the link (outside it - a control inside a link cannot be pressed without following it), opening a `.uin-rail-sub` `<ul>` of Sent / Drafts / Mentioned. Mentioned is omitted where `ownerUserId` is null. Open state is client `useState` plus a derived override from `current`, so a deep link to `sent:<id>` arrives expanded with no effect to keep in step; it is deliberately NOT in the query string. **"Asked me" renamed "Mentioned"** in the rail and in `viewTitle`. `styles.tsx`: `.uin-rail-branch`, `.uin-rail-twist` (18px, `rotate(90deg)` at `aria-expanded="true"`, `prefers-reduced-motion` honoured), `.uin-rail-sub` (indent + hairline, and `.uin-rail-sub[hidden] { display: none }` because the group's `display:flex` otherwise beats the `hidden` attribute), plus a lying-down variant under 1200px. `InboxPanel.tsx`: `folderAsked` / `folderInbox` / `folderIds` / `folderOwnerId` / `folderOwnerName` resolved once - **the id is checked against `visible` rather than trusted, and an id that is not on the list yields `[]` rather than falling back to everything**, which would be the E17 breach wearing a folder's name; `currentTab` gains the `folder:id` forms; `viewTitle` gains a "Name &middot; " prefix; the head now draws a title over a colleague's Mentioned tabs. `MentionListView.tsx` gains `ownerName` / `canSettle` - rows read "Marcus asked Sam", the empty state changes, and `MentionActions` is not rendered at all on somebody else's list (the route settles by user id regardless, so the buttons would have been refused). `InboxesPanel.tsx`: the individual branch of **Who can read it** is now a real guest list titled **Who else can read it**, owner excluded from the ticks and stated above them; list row gains a "Shared with N" chip and says so in its meta. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `npm test` 5654 passed / 421 files. **Live SQL run for real, both PASS, no skip:** `npm run test:inbox-mentions` 9 passed (NEW case: an ask list narrowed to one address, and another person's narrowed list not handing back the first person's), and `RUN_INBOX_SENT_GUARDS=1 vitest run modules/unified-inbox/lib/sent-list.live.test.ts` 10 passed (NEW `describe('the drafts folder')`, 4 cases, incl. the scoped shape that turns the unfiled half of the clause off) - both against real throwaway `cactus_rt_*` databases on the OVH VPS. Wiki `Unified-Inbox.md`: the "no guest list on an individual inbox" claim rewritten, NEW **Covering somebody's post** section, and the rail table corrected - it had been calling the SHARED group "Team inboxes", which is now a different group.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**A staff member's own phone number, a site dialling code, and the call modal rebuilt** - core + `unified-inbox` + `twilio`, uncommitted working tree. **CORE SCHEMA, two columns.** `User.phone TEXT NULL` - this person's own number, E.164, set on their account page. Deliberately NOT encrypted, unlike `smsOtpPhoneEncrypted` beside it: it is a contact detail rather than a credential, it is shown back to its owner in full, and a per-install-key column is unreadable after a restore into a fresh site. `SiteConfig.diallingCode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '+44'` - which country a number typed without one belongs to. Init migration edited in place AND new idempotent `prisma/core-reconcile/034_staff_phone_and_dialling_code.sql` (latest is now 034, not 033). NEW `lib/phone.ts` - pure, isomorphic, no DB: `toE164(input, diallingCode)` and `isE164`. Handles `+44…`, `0044…`, a single trunk `0`, and bare national digits; the ONE guess is bare digits that already start with the dialling code and leave >= 6 after it, which are read as international (a UK national number written without its trunk zero never starts 44). NEW `lib/phone.test.ts`, 25 cases. NEW `lib/phone.server.ts` - `siteDiallingCode()` + `DEFAULT_DIALLING_CODE`, alone in its own file exactly like `lib/config/timezone.server.ts`, because it touches Prisma and the arithmetic is wanted in the browser too. `app/api/account/profile/route.ts` GET now returns `phone` and `diallingCode`; PATCH takes `phone`, normalises it, 400s in plain English on nonsense, and returns the stored value so the box can show what was actually saved. `AccountPageClient.tsx` gains the Profile > **Your phone number** field (dirty tracking, unsaved-changes guard). `app/api/admin/config/route.ts` Patch gains `diallingCode` with a Zod transform accepting `+44` / `44` / `0044` and storing `+44`; `ConfigPageClient.tsx` shows it in the General > locale grid, which changes from `1fr 1fr 1fr` to `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 11rem), 1fr))` - the `min(100%, …)` form on purpose, a bare `minmax(11rem, 1fr)` blows out on a phone. **PADDING.** NEW `.admin-content--tight` in `app/globals.css` (`var(--space-4) var(--space-3)`, half of `--space-8`/`--space-6`; `--space-2` on mobile, half of `--space-4`), applied by `AdminShell` on the Inbox route via NEW exported `isInboxRoute` in `lib/nav/sidebar-rail-routes.ts` (`isSidebarRailRoute` now calls it). `app/cactus-admin/inbox/page.tsx` TabStrip margin 1.5rem -> 0.75rem. **`unified-inbox`:** `CallView.tsx` rebuilt - `defaultCallMeAt` / `diallingCode` / `accountHref` props, **Call me at** prefilled from `User.phone` and still editable, both number boxes tidied to E.164 `onBlur` (silently left alone when unreadable - never rewrite a half-typed number under the cursor), plain-English refusals naming both formats. `InboxPanel.tsx` reads `prisma.user.findUnique({phone})` + `siteDiallingCode()` in the same `Promise.all`, only when there is a number to call from. `app/api/calls/route.ts` re-runs `toE164` server-side and dials the normalised pair - the browser's tidy-up is a courtesy, not a guarantee. `styles.tsx`: NEW `.uin-field-row--stack` (label top-aligned, control column-flow, hint wraps and stays visible under 600px) used by all three call rows - the old layout put a nowrap sentence BESIDE the input, which is why **Call me at** was a centimetre wide; and `.uin-field-control input/select/textarea:focus-visible` now underlines (`box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0`) instead of inheriting the `.uin-modal …:focus-visible` outline box, which on a borderless full-width field landed on the hairlines above and below it. **`twilio`:** `lib/click-to-dial.ts` uses core's `toE164` with the site dialling code for the two TYPED numbers and drops its `normalisePhone` import; refusal copy rewritten. `normalisePhone` in `lib/verification.ts` is deliberately UNCHANGED and still strict - everywhere else in that module a number arrives from Twilio already in E.164, and loosening a webhook-facing validator to help a keyboard is how a wrong number gets dialled. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint` clean on every file this task touched, `npm test` 5638 passed / 420 files, `npm run test:backup-roundtrip` **4 passed** against real throwaway Postgres with all ten module schemas built. **`eslint .` across the whole tree is RED for a reason that is not this task:** another session's in-flight edit put backticks in a comment at `modules/unified-inbox/components/admin/inbox/styles.tsx:1338`, which ends the CSS template literal - the same trap logged two entries below. **Round-trip collision worth knowing:** the unified-inbox live suites provision `cactus_rt_uinint_*` / `cactus_rt_uinmrg_*` and `dropStaleTestObjects` drops EVERY `cactus_rt_%` object, so a concurrent run in another session deletes the round-trip's databases mid-test - it failed three times with `57P01` / "database does not exist" before a clear window let it pass. Nothing to do with the schema change.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Conversations can be merged into one, across addresses and across channels** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. NEW migration `modules/unified-inbox/migrations/031_thread_merges.sql` (another session took 032 for mentions - check `migrations/` before picking a number). **Schema:** `uin_threads.merged_into_id` (self FK ON DELETE SET NULL + partial index) marks the losing side, which is KEPT rather than deleted, exactly as `uin_people.merged_into_id` already does; `uin_messages.merged_from_thread_id` and `uin_drafts.merged_from_thread_id` (FK -> `uin_threads` SET NULL, partial indexes) stamp what a merge MOVED; NEW table `uin_thread_inboxes` (`thread_id`, `inbox_id`, PK on both, CASCADE both ways) is the set of addresses a merged conversation belongs to - EMPTY on every unmerged thread, and populated with the winner's own inbox as well as the losers', so the rule downstream is "rows here if any, `inbox_id` if none" rather than a union that would double-count; NEW table `uin_thread_merges` (`winner_id`, `loser_id`, `user_id`, `snapshot` jsonb, `undone_at`, `undone_by`) with NO foreign keys out, same reasoning as `uin_person_merges`. Both new tables added to `cactus.module.json` teardown - `uin_thread_inboxes` ahead of `uin_threads`, which `lib/teardown.test.ts` enforces. Types stay inside the backup serialiser's set (text / jsonb / timestamp(3)). **ACCESS CHANGE, the one to know about:** `ThreadShape` in `lib/access.ts` gains `absorbedInboxIds`, `threadAccessKind` now asks `effectiveInboxIds(thread).length > 0` rather than `thread.inboxId`, and both `canOpenThread` and `canUserOpenThread` return true if the reader can view ANY participating address. Merging across two addresses therefore WIDENS who can read the merged conversation - chosen deliberately (a merged conversation half the people on it cannot open is not one conversation), and the confirm dialog names every address before it happens. **Threading:** `ThreadRef` and `ThreadCandidate` in `lib/threading.ts` gain `absorbedInboxIds`; `pickThread` and the heuristic branch both go through the new `belongsToInbox()` instead of `===` on `inboxId`. This is what makes a merge survive the next reply - without it the side whose thread was merged away finds nothing to join and 020's split runs again. `threadsForMessageIds` and `candidateThreads` both now exclude `merged_into_id IS NOT NULL`: a merge deliberately LEAVES duplicate copies on the loser (they would collide with three unique indexes on the way across), and following one would file a reply onto a hidden thread. **NEW pure module `lib/thread-merge.ts`** - `pickWinner` (oldest wins, ties on id, so the same set merges the same way whatever order it was ticked), `effectiveInboxIds`, `belongsToInbox`, `mergedInboxIds`, `mergedStatus` (an open half makes the whole thing open), `mergedUnread`, `validateMerge`, `widenedAccessWarning`, `listSentence`, `mergeSummary`, `MAX_MERGE_SOURCES = 20`. 24 unit tests in `lib/thread-merge.test.ts`. **`lib/db.ts`:** new `ABSORBED_INBOX_IDS` SQL fragment and `inboxMatch()`; `visibilityClause` and `filterClauses` both go through `inboxMatch` so a merged conversation shows in EVERY address's tab, and `filterClauses` pushes `t."merged_into_id" IS NULL` for every list, count and status tally in one place; `unreadCounts` LEFT JOINs `uin_thread_inboxes` so it counts once under each address. New `threadsForMerge`, `mergeThreads`, `undoThreadMerge`, `undoableThreadMerges`, `getThreadMerge`, and the private `recomputeThreadCounters` / `recomputeThreadInboxes`. `ThreadRow` and `ThreadDetail` gain `absorbedInboxIds` + `mergedIntoId`; `ThreadListRow` gains `createdAt` (the merge screen needs to name which conversation wins) and `absorbedInboxIds`. `ThreadEventKind` gains `'unmerged'`. Merged-away threads excluded from `unresolvedThreads`, `threadsNeedingLinks`, `providerWatermarks`, `providerThreadsNeedingPeople`, `threadsDueForRetention`, `retentionDueCounts`, `countThreadsForPerson` and `listPeople`'s thread_count. **`providerThreadState` now FOLLOWS the pointer** - the module's own id for a chat stays on the side that lost the merge, so reading the row straight would hand the customer's next chat message to a hidden conversation and it would never appear. **Three GDPR/retention holes closed in the same change:** `deleteThreads` now also deletes messages whose `merged_from_thread_id` is in the set (otherwise erasing somebody leaves their words readable on another person's merged conversation) AND deletes threads merged INTO the ones being deleted (`merged_into_id` is ON DELETE SET NULL, so an orphaned loser would stop being merged away and reappear holding nothing but duplicates); `storedObjectsForThreads` and `exportMessagesForThreads` both follow `merged_from_thread_id`, the export attributing a moved message back to the conversation it came FROM; `personErasePreview` counts conversations with `FILTER (WHERE merged_into_id IS NULL)` so it agrees with the person's own page. **Merge mechanics:** one transaction (`timeout: 60_000`), losers processed ONE AT A TIME so the second loser's copy is weighed against a winner that has already taken the first's. A message the winner already holds is LEFT on the loser rather than deleted - three unique indexes decide that (`(connection_id, thread_id, message_id_header)`, `(thread_id, internal_key)`, `(thread_id, provider_message_id) WHERE source='provider'`) and deleting would make the undo a lie. Drafts likewise on `(thread_id, author_user_id)`. Duplicate record links ARE deleted (the linker puts them back), matching `mergePeople`. Chains are flattened: merging something that has itself absorbed others re-points every thread pointer aimed at it AND the `uin_thread_merges` rows naming it as winner - both halves or neither, since a thread pointing at the new winner whose merge record still names the old one is a merge whose undo hunts for its messages on a conversation they have left. Both sets of ids go in the snapshot. **Provenance across a nested merge was the subtle one:** the message move writes `merged_from_thread_id = COALESCE(src.merged_from_thread_id, loser)` rather than assigning, so a message an EARLIER merge moved keeps the conversation it actually started on - overwriting it made the inner merge un-undoable and exempted those messages from their own person's erasure, which finds them by that column. Undo therefore works off exact `messageIds` / `draftIds` in the snapshot rather than a WHERE on the column (a rule cannot tell this merge's rows from an earlier merge's riding along on the same conversation), and clears the column only `WHEN merged_from_thread_id = loser`. **NEW routes:** `app/api/threads/[id]/merge/route.ts` POST `{loserIds}` and `app/api/threads/merges/[id]/undo/route.ts` POST, both `unifiedinbox.manage`; the merge route checks `canOpenThread` on EVERY thread involved and returns the same refusal whether a thread is missing or unreadable. NEW `ThreadMergeBody` in `lib/validation.ts`. **UI:** `ThreadListView` gains a **Merge** button in the bulk bar (2+ picked, `canManage` only), a `mergePlan` memo using `pickWinner`/`widenedAccessWarning`, and a `ConfirmDialog`; NEW `components/admin/inbox/MergedFrom.tsx` ('use client') renders under `ThreadActions` with **Separate it again** per merge; `ThreadPane` gains `merges` and `otherInboxNames` props; `InboxPanel` loads `undoableThreadMerges` only when `canManage`. New styles `.uin-merged-from*` - tokens only, no hex. **NEW core script `npm run test:inbox-merge`** -> `modules/unified-inbox/lib/thread-merge.live.test.ts` (`RUN_INBOX_MERGE_GUARDS=1` + OVH creds; a skip is a FAIL), 18 cases against a real throwaway Postgres - none of this SQL is executed by `tsc`, `eslint`, `npm test` or the module build gate. **TRAP WORTH RECORDING:** `dropStaleTestObjects` in `lib/backup/vps-database.ts` drops EVERY `cactus_rt_*` database, not only the ones its own run created, so two agents running live suites against the OVH VPS at the same time destroy each other's throwaway databases mid-run - it surfaces as `57P01 terminating connection due to administrator command` or "Server has closed the connection" partway through applying the core schema, which reads exactly like a broken migration and is nothing of the kind. Check `SELECT datname FROM pg_database WHERE datname LIKE 'cactus_rt_%'` and the postgres log before believing it. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 803 passed / 0 failed. Wiki updated: new "Merging conversations into one", the permissions table, "Who can see what" and the people section.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The conversation's three controls moved onto the subject line, and two blocks of repetition came off the screen** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - markup, four components, one stylesheet, one adapter. `ThreadActions.tsx` no longer wraps itself in `.uin-actions` (a shared grid used by six other components, so it could not be made `display: contents`) and returns a fragment instead: one `.uin-thread-actions` row holding **alarm clock, Assign, status** in that order - Assign was on the left of a row of its own with the other two pushed right by `.uin-thread-actions-end`, which is **deleted** - plus the error alert as a sibling carrying the new `.uin-thread-actions-error` (`flex: 1 1 100%`) so it takes the line under the row rather than a slot in it. `ThreadPane` renders `<ThreadActions>` **inside `.uin-thread-top`**, between the `<h2>` and the close link, so the three sit left of the cross. `.uin-thread-subject` becomes a two-line clamp (`-webkit-line-clamp: 2` + `line-clamp: 2` + `-webkit-box-orient: vertical` + `overflow: hidden`) with `overflow-wrap: anywhere`, because a reference like `0000966554` is one word as far as wrapping goes and one word wider than the column pushes the buttons off the end. New `@media (max-width: 639px)` rule drops the group onto its own line - the three are about 13em together, which on a phone leaves 4em of subject. **`ThreadContext.tsx` loses the person entirely**: no name, no company, no `uin-thread-who` / `uin-thread-org` / `uin-thread-who-none` (markup and CSS both gone), no `person` / `noPersonReason` / `base` / `params` props, and `ThreadContextView` shrinks accordingly; `InboxPanel`'s `context={{...}}` drops two keys and its `reasonThereIsNobody()` helper is **deleted** as its only caller. What is left is the attached-records line. **`ContextAdapter.load` is now OPTIONAL** (`lib/adapters/types.ts`) and `loadContext` skips an adapter without one; `lib/adapters/purchase-orders.ts` drops its `load` and everything only it used (`OPEN_ORDER_STATUSES`, `UNSETTLED_BILL_STATUSES`, `SECTION_LIMIT`, `toDate`, the `ContextItem`/`ContextQuery`/`ContextSection` imports), so the **Purchasing** block no longer appears in the context rail on a conversation OR on a person's page. Its `lookup` and `suggest` are untouched: PO numbers are still spotted in messages, still auto-attached and still offered by the attach picker. `tables` narrows to `['po_suppliers', 'po_orders']` - `po_bills` is no longer read from here, so it is no longer a condition of spotting a number. **Trap re-hit:** a backtick inside a comment in `styles.tsx` ends the CSS template literal - `tsc` reports it as `TS1005: ',' expected` on a CSS line, which is not obviously the same thing. Wiki updated: **Assign**, the status button and the alarm clock's positions, the "What the conversation is about" section (was "Who you are talking to, and what it is about"), and the beside-a-conversation panel's list. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean, `eslint .` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox/lib/adapters` 10 passed. **Another session was editing this same worktree throughout** - `InboxPanel.tsx` and `ThreadPane.tsx` both gained `merges` / `otherInboxNames` mid-task; every edit here was a fresh read-then-write, but check `git diff` before staging.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Tagging a colleague in an internal note is now a job with its own lifecycle, and a way in** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. NEW migration `modules/unified-inbox/migrations/032_mentions.sql` - **numbered 032, not 031**, because another session in this same worktree is mid-flight on `031_thread_merges.sql`; check `migrations/` before picking a number. One table `uin_mentions` (`id`, `thread_id`, `message_id`, `user_id`, `by_user_id`, `note`, `status` open|snoozed|done, `snooze_until`, `settled_at`, timestamps), UNIQUE `(thread_id, user_id)` - one job per person per conversation, so being asked twice REOPENS the first rather than stacking a second. FKs: thread CASCADE, message SET NULL, `user_id` -> `User` CASCADE, `by_user_id` -> `User` SET NULL. Added to `cactus.module.json` teardown ahead of `uin_attachments`. Types stay inside the backup serialiser's set (text / timestamp(3)). **ACCESS CHANGE, the important one:** `ThreadShape` in `lib/access.ts` gains an optional `id`, and `canOpenThread` / `canUserOpenThread` now fall through to `hasMentionOn(threadId, userId)` when the guest list refuses - so a tag GRANTS that one conversation to that one person. Narrow on purpose: one conversation not the inbox, handed over only by somebody who could already open it, READING only (`canReplyToInbox` never consults it), and still gated on holding `unifiedinbox.view` (new exported `userCanOpenHub`). `InboxPanel`'s hand-rolled copy of that rule is deleted and calls `canOpenThread`; `getMessageHtml` now returns `threadId` so the two `messages/[id]/...` routes can pass it; `attachments/[id]` gets a `hasMentionOn` second chance so a tagged colleague can open the invoice they were asked about. NEW route `app/api/mentions/[id]/route.ts` PATCH `{status, snoozeUntil}` - `setMentionStatus` scopes by `user_id` IN THE UPDATE, so one colleague cannot settle another's; 404 when nothing matched. NEW db helpers in `lib/db.ts`: `hasMentionOn`, `upsertMention`, `listMentions`, `countMentions`, `mentionStatusCounts`, `openMentionCount`, `mentionForThread`, `setMentionStatus`, `wakeDueMentions` (+ `MentionRow`, `MentionStatusFilter`). `lib/mentions.ts` rewritten - `notifyMentions` writes rows and takes `messageId`/`note`, plus a pure exported `whoToTell` (dedupes, drops self); the old `canUserOpenThread` gate is GONE, replaced by the grant. NEW `MentionPatchBody` in `lib/validation.ts`. NEW param `mentionsOnly` (`?inbox=mentions`) in `lib/list.ts`, excluded from the `inboxId` fallthrough. UI: NEW `components/admin/inbox/MentionActions.tsx` ('use client', reuses `Dropdown` + `SnoozePanel`) and `MentionListView.tsx` (RSC). `NoteBar` gains a `staff` prop and an @ button revealing a chip picker - tagging used to be reachable only from the full composer. `ThreadPane` gains an `asked: AskedView | null` prop and a `.uin-asked` banner above the held-draft warnings, passes `staff` to `NoteBar`, and its event log names WHO was asked (`eventWords`). `NavRail` gains `askedCount` and an **Asked me** entry beside Assigned to me. `StatusTabs` gains optional `unit` / `ariaLabel` so the same four tabs serve asks. `icons.tsx` gains `AtIcon`. New styles `.uin-ask*`, `.uin-asked*`, `.uin-notebar-tag*`, `.uin-notebar-tags`, `.uin-notebar-find`, `.uin-notebar-tagnote` - tokens only, no hex. Snoozed asks wake in `InboxPanel` (beside `wakeDueThreads`) and in the housekeeping cron. NEW core script `npm run test:inbox-mentions` -> `modules/unified-inbox/lib/mentions.live.test.ts` (`RUN_INBOX_MENTION_GUARDS=1` + OVH creds; a skip is a FAIL). **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean except two pre-existing errors in another session's untracked `lib/thread-merge.test.ts`; `eslint modules/unified-inbox` clean; `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 803 passed / 0 failed; live mention SQL suite 8 passed against a real throwaway Postgres.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Answering moved onto the message; the conversation's own controls became controls** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - markup, five components and one stylesheet. NEW `components/admin/inbox/Dropdown.tsx` ('use client'): the one popover the screen now uses five times over - `position: fixed` and placed off the trigger's rect (the header it hangs from is sticky inside `.uin-read`, which scrolls its own contents, so an absolutely-positioned panel is a clipped one), measured with `useLayoutEffect` so a 400px calendar opens upwards rather than off the bottom, dismissed on outside pointerdown / Escape / scroll / resize, arrow keys walking only `[data-menu-item]`. Its `close` reaches the contents through **context** (`useDropdownClose`), NOT as a render-prop argument: calling `children(close)` during render makes `react-hooks/refs` a hard error, because `close` reads the trigger's ref. `MenuItem` shuts the panel itself unless given `keepOpen` (the two entries that swap what is in the panel rather than choosing anything: Day & Time, and FilterMenu's Assignees). NEW `MessageMenu.tsx` - a reply arrow and a three-dot menu (Reply all / Forward / Mark as unread) at the trailing end of EVERY `.uin-msg-head`, handed into `ThreadPane`'s `Message`/`MessageHeader` as a `tools: ReactNode` prop so the pane stays an RSC. `ReplyActions` is **deleted** from `ComposerOpen.tsx` and `useComposerOpen` exported in its place; `cannotReplyReason` now renders as `.uin-thread-cannot` under the actions row. The dots menu carries a fourth entry, **Internal note**, ONLY when `canReply` is false: on a discussion there is no reply arrow at all, so without it the full note box - attachments, mentions - would be unreachable and the one-line bar would be the whole of what a discussion could say. NEW `NoteBar.tsx` - a one-line internal-note box, `position: sticky; bottom: 0`, last child of `.uin-thread`, which gains `.uin-thread-conv` (`grid-template-rows: auto 1fr auto`) so the bar sits at the foot of the pane on a short thread; the class had to be a modifier because `ContactCard`, `OrganisationCard`, `ContactImport` and `PersonView` all render plain `.uin-thread`. It posts the existing `/threads/[id]/notes` with no mentions; the full composer keeps note mode, mentions and attachments behind any reply arrow. NEW `SnoozePanel.tsx` - the three `snoozeOptions` with the day and time each lands on written beside them, then **Day & Time**: a six-row Monday-first month with prev/next, native date and time boxes, Cancel/Snooze. Its instant comes from core's `instantAtWallClock(date, time, timezone)`, so the wall clock is the SITE's - the old `datetime-local` box built `new Date(value)` in the BROWSER's zone, which was an hour out for anyone not standing in it. The month's name is built from a `MONTHS` table, never by formatting a `Date` in the site zone: midnight on the first read a few hours west is the last day of the month before. `ThreadActions.tsx` rewritten - **Assign** (was "With nobody yet") on the left, and at the right an alarm-clock icon plus a status button reading Open/Snoozed/Done with a chevron, whose menu offers the statuses it is not; the standalone read/unread button is gone, that job having moved to the message's own dots. `icons.tsx` gains `MoreIcon`, `AlarmIcon`, `ChevronLeftIcon`, `ChevronRightIcon`. New styles: `.uin-dropdown`, `.uin-menu*`, `.uin-icon-btn` (+`-framed`), `.uin-msg-tools`, `.uin-thread-cannot`, `.uin-thread-actions-end`, `.uin-status-btn`, `.uin-cal*`, `.uin-notebar*`, `.uin-thread-conv`. Tokens only, no hex. **Touched another session's in-flight file:** `FilterMenu.tsx` (untracked, being written in this same worktree) was built against `Dropdown`'s render-prop shape and against a `MenuItem` that did not close itself, so it was moved to `useDropdownClose()` and its Assignees entry given `keepOpen` - three lines, needed for `tsc` to be green at all. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` in the module clean, `npm run typecheck` in core clean (0 errors), `eslint .` clean, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 773 passed / 0 failed. No new component has a test either before or after.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Who this is, and what it is about, moved into the conversation's own header** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. No schema, route, permission or API change - markup and one prop. The **Who this is** block was the first block of `ContextRail`, which on anything narrower than 1500px is the panel UNDERNEATH the messages: the answer to "who am I talking to" sat below however many thousand pixels of quoted email the thread had. It is now two lines at the foot of `.uin-thread-head`, which is `position: sticky` from 900px up, so it is always on the screen. NEW `components/admin/inbox/ThreadContext.tsx` (RSC) - the name (a link to that person's own view, via `inboxHref`) with the company name after it behind a hairline rather than a middot in the markup, since punctuation between two names is read out as punctuation; falls back to `reasonThereIsNobody(thread.channel)` when the channel never had a person. NEW `components/admin/inbox/AttachedRecords.tsx` ('use client') - the second line: every attached record's label comma-joined ("Purchase order PO-0023, Order DW0234"), ONE line, `text-overflow: ellipsis`, with the whole list in the `title`. The chevron beside it opens the manage menu (each record as a link, `LinkActions` to take one off, `AddLink` to put one on), which is `position: fixed` and placed off the trigger's rect for the same reason `ComposeMenu` is - the header is pinned inside `.uin-read`, which scrolls its own contents, so an absolutely-positioned menu is a clipped menu. One deliberate difference from `ComposeMenu`: its scroll/resize dismissal ignores events whose target is inside the menu, because `.uin-ctx-picker` is itself a scrolling list. NEW pure `lib/record-links.ts` (`recordLabel`, `recordHref`) - lifted verbatim out of `ContextRail` so a client island and an RSC can share one table instead of two that drift. `ContextRail` accordingly loses `base`, `params`, `person`, `noPersonReason`, `linkKinds` and `defaultLinkKind`, and draws the attached block only when `threadId` is null (a person's page); beside a conversation it is now the adapter sections alone, and `InboxPanel` renders it only when there IS a person, so a channel with nobody on it collapses the fourth column instead of holding one sentence about nobody. `ThreadPane` gains one `context: ThreadContextView` prop; the person/links/kinds load in `InboxPanel` moved above the `ThreadPane` element so both can be fed from it - same four queries, same `Promise.all`, no extra round trip. New styles: `.uin-thread-ctx`, `.uin-thread-who`, `.uin-thread-org`, `.uin-thread-who-none`, `.uin-attached`, `.uin-attached-line`, `.uin-attached-more`, `.uin-attached-menu` (its 300px width is repeated as `MENU_WIDTH` in the component - keep them in step). Tokens only, no hex. **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 errors, `eslint .` clean, `lib/modules/client-graph.test.ts` + `lib/db/client-bundle.test.ts` pass. No test covers these components either before or after.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Not everything is an email: a discussion, a call and a text from the write button** - `core` + `twilio` + `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. NEW CORE SEAM `core.dialler` (`lib/dialler/types.ts` - `Dialler`, `DiallerNumber`, `DialRequest`, `DialResult`; `lib/dialler/registry.ts` - `resolveDiallers`, `firstDialler`, `callerNumbers`), shaped exactly like `lib/conversations/providers.ts`: same `INSTALLED_MODULE_WHERE` gate, permission filter and shape check. Two-leg by contract - `DialRequest.callMeAt` is required - and a refusal is `{ ok: false, reason }` rather than a throw. `firstDialler` is the CHEAP question (database only, for deciding whether to draw a button); `callerNumbers` reaches the telephony API and belongs to the screen that opens. `twilio` publishes it (`lib/dialler.ts` -> `twilioDialler`, manifest entry `twilio-dialler`, `serverOnly: true`), and the two-leg TwiML that was inline in `app/api/admin/twilio/make-call/route.ts` moved to `lib/click-to-dial.ts` so the settings screen and the seam place the identical call with the identical caller-ID checks; the route keeps its 503-for-unconfigured / 502-for-thrown split via `result.code`. `twilio` 0.1.34 -> 0.1.35, `requiresCoreVersion` -> 0.5.1534. UNIFIED-INBOX: `ComposeMenu.tsx` ('use client') turns the rail's pen into a split button - the pen still writes an email, the arrow opens Discussion / Call / SMS. The menu is `position: fixed` and placed off the trigger's rect because `.uin-rail-scroll` scrolls (a column at >=1200px, a strip with `overflow-y: hidden` below), and an absolutely-positioned menu inside it loses its bottom half on a phone; it closes on scroll, resize, Escape and any pointerdown outside. Entries are omitted when the site cannot do them, and with none there is no arrow at all. `lib/list.ts` gains `ComposeKind` and `parseComposeKind` - `compose=1` still means the email composer, `discussion`/`sms`/`call` are new, anything else opens nothing (the pre-existing contract, tested). New `components/admin/inbox/ComposeModal.tsx` carries the dialog chrome the three short forms share (focus trap, Escape, scroll lock, the you-have-typed-something question) as a render-prop; `ComposeView` keeps its own, being a screenful rather than a form. DISCUSSION = a thread with `channel = 'discussion'` (migration `030_discussions.sql` widens the `uin_threads_channel_check` by DROP IF EXISTS + ADD; `001_initial.sql` edited in place to match), made of the internal notes the module already had. `createDiscussionThread` opens it UNREAD - the one place it differs from a note, because a conversation carries one unread flag between everybody and a discussion nobody is told about is one nobody reads. `POST /api/m/unified-inbox/discussions` takes `unifiedinbox.view` (a note leaves the building through no door), checks `canViewInbox` for EVERY named address BEFORE writing anything, and starts one discussion per address - one thread cannot honestly have two guest lists, two unread counts and one `inbox_id`, and this is the same answer `020_internal_threads.sql` already gave. `InboxPanel` forces `canReply = false` on a discussion so the composer opens on the note box rather than a Reply that would refuse. Mention notification extracted to `lib/mentions.ts` and the notes route refactored onto it. SMS = `POST /api/m/unified-inbox/sms` over core's existing `getActiveSmsProvider()` / `normaliseSmsNumber`; CALL = `POST /api/m/unified-inbox/calls` over the new seam. NEITHER WRITES A ROW: the module that owns texts and calls publishes them straight back as a `core.conversation-provider` phone conversation, so a second copy here would be the same message twice with only one of them knowing whether it was delivered. New pure `lib/sms-segments.ts` (160/70, one curly quote shortens the whole message) with 8 tests; `lib/db.ts` gains `latestPhoneOnThread` (inbound only - `from_phone` on our own outgoing message is our own number). CORE FIX en route: `scripts/generate-module-router.mjs` typed `ApiHandlerModule` as a map of handlers, so every route exporting `maxDuration` or `dynamic` was a type error in a generated file nobody can edit - 25 of them, all pre-existing. Now `Record<string, unknown>` with the handler narrowed at the one place it is read. **Gates:** `npm run typecheck` clean (0 errors, down from 25), `eslint .` clean, `npm test` 5594 passed / 0 failed. No new table, column, sequence or type, so no backup round-trip - the only DDL is a widened CHECK constraint, which a fresh restore builds from the migrations anyway.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**Search everything, from the head of the rail** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. New `components/admin/inbox/InboxSearch.tsx` ('use client'): a magnifier button in `.uin-rail-me`, LEFT of the compose control, opening a dialog over the whole hub - Ctrl/Cmd K opens it from anywhere in the inbox, Escape and the backdrop close it, focus is trapped and restored to the boxes on each open. Six new query params, all read defensively in `lib/list.ts` `parseInboxParams`: `from`, `to`, `subject`, `att=1`, `after`, `before` (calendar dates validated properly - `2026-02-31` is rejected rather than rolled into March). New pure helpers in `lib/list.ts`: `SearchRequest`/`EMPTY_SEARCH`, `buildSearchHref` (defaults scope `all` and status `all`, drops `page`/`id`/`person`/`compose`/`draft`, and takes ONLY the words to the address book), `searchRequestFrom` (Drafts/Sent/Contacts/Campaigns are not scopes, so a search opened over one looks everywhere), `isSearching`, `formatCalendarDate`. `lib/db.ts` `ThreadListFilters` gains `fromText`/`toText`/`subjectText`/`withAttachment`/`after`/`before` and `filterClauses` an EXISTS per cut - one per conversation rather than one subquery for all of them, because "from the supplier" and "with something attached" are usually two different messages of the same thread. `to` matches `array_to_string(to_addresses || cc_addresses, ' ')` so a copied-in address counts. New exported `likeContains` escapes `\`, `%` and `_` - unescaped, a search for `%` matched every row. Dates are compared against `last_message_at`; `InboxPanel.tsx` turns the two calendar dates into instants with `instantAtWallClock(..., '00:00'/'24:00', timezone)`, so "up to and including" means the whole of that day in the SITE's zone rather than in UTC. `Filters.tsx` gains a `narrowed` prop and a clear-chip per active cut; `ThreadListView` `searching` now comes from `isSearching(params)` so a date range that catches nothing says "nothing matches that" rather than "nothing here". Every cut is ANDed inside the one WHERE with the visibility clause (E17) - a conversation in an inbox the reader cannot open is never fetched, counted or paged. **Gates:** new `modules/unified-inbox/lib/search-sql.live.test.ts`, `RUN_INBOX_SEARCH_SQL=1`, wired as `npm run test:inbox-search-sql` - 9 cases against a real throwaway `cactus_rt_uinsearch_*` Postgres (the words, the wildcard escaping, cc counting as "to", the subject on thread and message, attachments, the date range's inclusive far end, all cuts together, `statusCounts` under them, and the E17 leak). **9/9 real PASS.** 9 new pure cases in `lib/list.test.ts`. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` clean on every touched file, `vitest run modules/unified-inbox` 772 passed / 0 failed. No migration, no schema change, so no backup round-trip needed.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**The inbox's three columns can be dragged** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. New `components/admin/inbox/column-widths.ts` (pure: `DEFAULT_WIDTHS` 240/384, `WIDTH_LIMITS`, `MIN_READ` 360, `CTX_WIDTH` 256, `clampWidth`, `roomForColumns`, `settleWidths`, `parseStoredWidths`) and `components/admin/inbox/ColumnResizer.tsx` ('use client'), mounted last inside the frame in `InboxPanel.tsx` and again with `handles={false}` on both campaigns frames. It writes `--uin-w-rail` / `--uin-w-list` onto **`document.documentElement`**, not onto the frame: the campaigns screen is a second `.uin-app` with the same rail, and a var set on the frame made the rail spring back to 15rem on the way between the two screens. `styles.tsx` now reads `--uin-rail: var(--uin-w-rail, 15rem)` / `--uin-list: var(--uin-w-list, 24rem)` in the >=1200px block and uses them in the 3-column, 4-column and `.uin-app-wide` templates - fallbacks are plain lengths on purpose, because the handles' `left` is `calc()`ed off them and a `minmax()` fallback would break that. 24rem is what the old `minmax(17rem, 24rem)` list always resolved to beside a 1fr reading pane, so nothing moves until somebody drags. Handles are `position: absolute` inside the frame (which is now `position: relative` below 900px and sticky above), 9px wide, `z-index: 4` (over the sticky thread head at 3, under the drop overlay at 5), `display: none` below 1200px - below that the rail is a strip across the top and a full-height handle would eat its clicks. `role=separator` + `aria-valuenow/min/max`, arrow keys (Shift = 48px), Home resets, double-click resets; widths in `localStorage` under `uin-column-widths`, read after mount so the server-rendered frame does not trip hydration. `settleWidths` only ever gives way on the edge being dragged, never the other one. Tests: `column-widths.test.ts`, 9 cases. Gates: `eslint` clean on every touched file; `npm run typecheck` shows only the 25 pre-existing generated-`lib/modules/router.ts` errors plus one transient `InboxPanel.tsx` line-936 `NavRail` prop mismatch belonging to another session's in-flight discussions work in this same tree.)

Last updated: 2026-09-06 (**An inbox is now either the business’s or one person’s** - `unified-inbox`, uncommitted working tree. Front's distinction, and the only place on this site where holding `unifiedinbox.manage` is NOT a way past a list. Migration `029_inbox_kind.sql` adds `uin_inboxes.kind` (TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'shared', CHECK IN ('individual','shared')) and `owner_user_id` (TEXT, FK -> `User(id)` **ON DELETE SET NULL**, indexed). No dollar-quoting anywhere - constraints are DROP IF EXISTS then ADD rather than a DO block, because a `$$` pair takes the whole module out of the backup round-trip. **Nothing is converted:** every existing address becomes 'shared', which is what they all already behaved as; turning one into somebody's own is a deliberate act in Settings, since it takes an address away from people who can read it today. `decideInboxAccess` gained a leading `inbox: {kind, ownerUserId}` argument and now branches on the kind BEFORE the manage short-circuit - individual: owner only (and they still need `unifiedinbox.view`), everybody else false including a manager; owner NULL (their account was deleted) falls back to manage so the post is not sealed in. Shared is byte-for-byte the old rule. `canViewInbox` / `canReplyToInbox` / `canUserViewInbox` / `canUserReplyToInbox` lost their manage short-circuits and read the new `getInboxAudience(id)`; `visibleInboxIds` / `replyableInboxIds` share a new private `audienceIndex()` (`listAllInboxAccess` + new `listInboxAudiences()`, two queries for the whole site, never two per address). New pure `audienceForSave()` forces an individual inbox's guest list to exactly its owner - written redundantly to `uin_inbox_access` AND `uin_user_default_inbox` so any query that reads the guest list and has never heard of `kind` still gets the right answer. New `lib/inbox-kind.ts`: pure `kindProblem()` (individual needs an owner; **individual may not be the catch-all**, because unplaceable post filed into a private mailbox is invisible to the person who has to sort it out), `ownerProblem()` (refuses a deleted or suspended account) and `settleIndividualAudience()`, called by BOTH inbox routes straight after the row is written - the guest list arrives as a second request and that request can fail. The PATCH route judges the kind on the MERGE of before+patch, so turning on the catch-all without mentioning the kind is refused from either end; the access PUT re-applies `audienceForSave` so a stale screen cannot put the old team back on a personal inbox. `inboxIsSomebodysOwn` is now a UNION ALL of the default-inbox row and `kind='individual'`. UI: new **Whose inbox it is** radio group in `InboxesPanel` with an owner picker, the guest-list group replaced by a sentence on a personal one, the catch-all tick hidden, a Personal/Shared chip on every list row. `TabInbox` gained `kind`; new pure `splitInboxes()` in `lib/list.ts` (built on `pinDefaultInbox`: an individual inbox is under Yours by its nature, a pinned shared one by preference) and the rail's second group is now **Shared inboxes** - only those drag. A thread in an individual inbox offers only its owner in the **With** menu. 12 new tests across `access.test.ts`, `inbox-kind.test.ts` and `default-inbox.test.ts`. `npm run typecheck` unchanged at 25 pre-existing errors in generated `lib/modules/router.ts` (verified against a clean tree), `eslint modules/unified-inbox` clean, 739 module tests pass - the single failure in `list.test.ts` is ANOTHER session's in-flight `parseComposeKind` work in the shared module worktree, not this change. **Backup gate not run:** the round-trip needs the OVH credentials exported and the server quiet; the schema-coverage backstop is green and the migration adds TEXT columns only, which it already has a branch for.)
